**[00:11:03.450] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000054-split-3)

**[00:11:06.440] Rep. Manuel "Wowo" L. Fortes Jr.** (asr-000054-split-4)

**[00:12:17.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000056-split-3)  
Is there any objection? The Chair hears none. The period of sponsorship and debate is now open, and we recognize the sponsor

**[00:12:23.120] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000057)  
The budget of the Commission on Human Rights, in the person of Vice Chairperson of Appropriations Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez of Alona Partylist. You may now proceed.

**[00:12:35.230] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000057-split-2)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues in the House of Representatives, magandang gabi po sa ating lahat. I rise today to sponsor the fiscal year 2027 budget of the Commission on Human Rights, an independent constitutional institution trusted with protecting the dignity and fundamental rights of every Filipino.

**[00:12:53.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000058)  
Madam Speaker, allow me to put the CHR budget in perspective through one figure: 0.02%. Out of proposed 7.2 trillion national budget, only PHP 1.273 billion, or around 0.02%, is provided for the CHR under the National Expenditure Program. The Commission proposed 2.749 billion.

**[00:13:23.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000059)  
Even if Congress were to provide the entire amount, it would only represent around 0.04% of the national budget, from 0.02% to 0.04%. Napakaliit po ng hinihingi ng CHR, but it can make a meaningful difference in its ability to investigate human rights violations, assist victims, prevent abuse, and protect the rights of our people.

**[00:13:53.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000060)  
We cannot continue asking the CHR to do more without giving it the resources necessary to fulfill its constitutional mandate. The CHR is not an adversary of government. Kakampi po natin ang CHR sa mabuting pamamahala. A stronger and independent CHR strengthens accountability, protects the vulnerable, and helps build public trust in our institution. Madam Speaker,

**[00:14:23.120] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000061)  
0.1% is a modest investment for our institution mandated to safeguard the rights of 100% of our Filipino people. For this reason, I respectfully urge this august chamber to support the approval and augmentation of the fiscal 2027 budget of the Commission on Human Rights. Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker, and I am now ready to take questions from our distinguished

**[00:14:52.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000062)  
colleagues.

**[00:14:53.020] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000064)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:15:00.000] Rep. Manuel "Wowo" L. Fortes Jr.** (asr-000065)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Iris Marie de Mesa Montes of 4K Partylist to interpellate on the proposed budget.

**[00:15:09.220] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000066)  
Representative Iris Marie de Mesa Montes of 4K Partylist is now recognized for her interpolation.

**[00:15:15.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000067)  
Thank you Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, the Commission on Human Rights Family. Magandang gabi po sa inyong lahat.

**[00:15:23.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000068)  
Unang katanungan ko po, would the Commission agree that the credibility of the Philippines' human rights institutions is itself an important component of the country's credibility in the international community?

**[00:15:39.780] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000069)  
Yes po Madam Speaker.

**[00:15:40.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000070)  
Thank you Madam Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[00:15:45.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000071)  
The recent failure of our country's bid for a non-permanent seat in the United Nations Security Council,

**[00:15:53.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000072)  
losing

**[00:15:53.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000073)  
the Asia-Pacific contest to Kyrgyzstan in June 2026 has been interpreted by some as a human rights warning.

**[00:16:01.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000074)  
In fact, prominent international human rights organizations including the International Coalition for Human Rights in the Philippines

**[00:16:08.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000075)  
have publicly interpreted this electoral setback as a direct reflection of ongoing concerns regarding our domestic human rights record,

**[00:16:19.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000076)  
especially during the Duterte administration.

**[00:16:23.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000077)  
Does the Sponsor share this view?

**[00:16:27.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000078)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[00:16:30.500] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000079)  
What role exactly did this unresolved human rights violation, especially in the past administration, play in this outcome?

**[00:17:07.360] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000080)  
Madam Chair,

**[00:17:09.040] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000081)  
one of the Commission's significant accomplishments has been the implementation of the Task Force on Zero Backlog Cases,

**[00:17:16.100] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000082)  
which substantially reduced the number of pending cases and helped clean up and reconcile our case data.

**[00:17:23.240] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000083)  
In particular,

**[00:17:23.920] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000084)  
the Commission has identified 493 cases for reinvestigation in 2024,

**[00:17:30.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000085)  
and the Task Force has directed of 289 cases reinvestigated in 2025.

**[00:17:37.700] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000086)  
Of these 289 reinvestigated cases, 140 cases are currently for disposition by the TF.

**[00:17:45.540] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000087)  
The Task Force also successfully reviewed and evaluated these cases identified for reinvestigation,

**[00:17:52.540] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000088)  
and has begun drafting the corresponding resolution for disposition.

**[00:17:57.720] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000089)  
This process remains ongoing, with the Commission committed to completing the disposition of these cases by the end of the year, Madam Chair.

**[00:18:07.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000090)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[00:18:10.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000091)  
Yung mga binanggit niyo po ay mga accomplishments po ba yan ng CHR?

**[00:18:16.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000092)  
Yes po, Madam Chair.

**[00:18:17.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000093)  
But I was actually trying to find out if, when I asked a while ago,

**[00:18:22.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000094)  
if the Human Rights Record of the Philippines, as seen by the international community,

**[00:18:28.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000095)  
had something to do with our lost bid to the UNSC,

**[00:18:34.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000096)  
and to which you said in the affirmative,

**[00:18:36.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000097)  
ang gusto ko pong malaman nga,

**[00:18:39.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000098)  
ano po itong mga unresolved cases in the past administration that you think ay nakakapag-contribute doon sa paningin na iyon ng international community?

**[00:18:57.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000099)  
Madam Chair, Representative Montes,

**[00:19:02.700] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000100)  
for example po, ang ating pong mga naka-accomplish po ay katulad po ng red tagging and EJA.

**[00:19:08.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000101)  
Okay, thank you, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues.

**[00:19:14.200] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000102)  
Yung mga ganyan po, nabanggit niyo po yung mga red tagging EJK,

**[00:19:20.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000103)  
has the Commission

**[00:19:22.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000104)  
examined

**[00:19:23.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000105)  
Whether these concerns ng international community, consistent po ba yan sa findings ng Human Rights Commission?

**[00:19:31.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000106)  
Po, ano po yung iniisip ng international community?

**[00:19:35.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000107)  
Yan din po ba ang paningin ng CHR?

**[00:19:40.260] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000108)  
Yes po, Madam Chair.

**[00:19:42.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000109)  
Okay po. Maraming salamat po.

**[00:19:46.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000110)  
Can CHR identify programs, especially in the 2027 budget

**[00:19:53.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000111)  
that have demonstrated measurable improvements in compliance with human rights standards?

**[00:20:01.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000112)  
Ano po yung mga programang nakapaloob dyan sa 2027 budget natin na magpapakita o magpapatunay na meron nga from the past administration, meron po tayong improvement ngayon?

**[00:20:16.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000113)  
A human rights-centered national protection response mechanism.

**[00:20:20.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000114)  
It integrates

**[00:20:22.360] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000115)  
early warning, victim supports, and rapid response mechanism.

**[00:20:26.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000116)  
So, meron po tayo ng mga tinatawag po nating programa, katulad po ng Bantay Karapatang Pantao,

**[00:20:33.140] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000117)  
which detect, monitor, and prevent human rights risks through data and intelligence.

**[00:20:39.140] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000118)  
Kalinga Karapatang Pantao provides holistic, victim-centered support, protection, and recovery services.

**[00:20:46.320] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000119)  
At syempre po, meron din po tayong Tugon Karapatang Pantao, which responds quickly, coordinates,

**[00:20:52.540] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000120)  
intervention, and ensure protection and accountability.

**[00:20:56.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000121)  
Madam Chair.

**[00:20:57.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000122)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[00:21:01.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000123)  
Let me move on to my next question.

**[00:21:04.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000124)  
Just this morning, during the budget deliberations of the Dangerous Drugs Board,

**[00:21:11.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000125)  
the Sponsor, Congressman Marcy Cudoro, made mention of the necessity to include the Commission on Human Rights

**[00:21:18.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000126)  
as a part of the Board of the Dangerous Drugs Board.

**[00:21:22.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000127)  
If ever we include the CHR in the DDB, of course, by way of an amendment to the law creating the DDB,

**[00:21:34.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000128)  
how do you envision your role in shaping drug-related policy?

**[00:21:41.340] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000129)  
Madam Chair, alam niyo po, mandato po ng CHR, lahat po ng karapatang pantao ay lagi po natin ang gagampanan.

**[00:21:48.340] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000130)  
Pero doon po sa, kanina po sa Dangerous Drugs Board,

**[00:21:52.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000131)  
siyempre po, more than willing po na makisama, makisuporta ang CHR,

**[00:21:59.020] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000132)  
at magkaroon po kami ng sarili natin position paper sa lahat po ng mga cases na drug-related po.

**[00:22:09.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000133)  
Actually, Madam Chair, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[00:22:14.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000134)  
what the DDB, what the sponsor of the DDB budget really wanted was to include the CHR

**[00:22:22.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000135)  
in the board as to help in formulating policies, kasi policymaking po yung Dangerous Drugs Board,

**[00:22:34.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000136)  
to help in the formulation of policies.

**[00:22:36.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000137)  
So, alam po natin naging issue yung mga EJK, mga drugs in the past.

**[00:22:44.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000138)  
So, I think it really is necessary to include the CHR there.

**[00:22:52.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000139)  
So, halimbawa po, kasi I'm looking at, I'm willing to sponsor a bill tungkol po dyan.

**[00:23:02.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000140)  
I'm looking at, for instance, in strengthening oversight sa human rights in drugs enforcement strategies.

**[00:23:09.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000141)  
Ano ang pwedeng maitulong ng CHR in that regard?

**[00:23:14.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000142)  
Okay. Sagutin ko po muna yung unang question nyo po ulit, Madam Chair.

**[00:23:19.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000143)  
The DDB, no. The DDB has not included.

**[00:23:22.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000144)  
The DDB has not engaged with CHR, but the CHR is more willing to be an observer po.

**[00:23:27.720] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000145)  
With regards po sa drug-related po, CHR proposes that drug use to be treated as a public health issue.

**[00:23:35.900] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000146)  
Kasi po, drug use is currently being over-criminalized where users who need services to address their dependence or use are instead treated as criminals and jails.

**[00:23:47.140] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000147)  
Drug cases contribute to more than 70% of the jails.

**[00:23:52.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000148)  
So, sa lahat po ng mga pinupuntahan po ng CHR, Madam Chair, 70% po ang mga nakakulong po sa ating mga PDLs po natin.

**[00:24:03.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000149)  
Yeah. What you said was as observer. Is that correct? Did I hear you right?

**[00:24:09.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000150)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[00:24:11.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000151)  
Hindi po, hindi po sang-ayon na maging permanent seat sa board yung CHR.

**[00:24:19.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000152)  
Kasi kung ang pagbabasehan po natin,

**[00:24:22.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000153)  
nung kung nga po una, kung naka-apekto yung mga EJK, mga problema ng human rights violations doon sa drugs,

**[00:24:30.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000154)  
hindi po ba dapat nandodon din ang CHR?

**[00:24:35.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000155)  
Kasi kung may mga usapin po tayo ng paglabag ng human rights doon sa mga biktima ng mga EJKs.

**[00:24:43.020] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000156)  
Madam Chair, ulitin po namin, we cannot because we have to maintain our independence po.

**[00:24:49.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000157)  
Okay.

**[00:24:52.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000158)  
Ibig sabihin po, kung sakasakali, your participation would only be limited to being an observer

**[00:24:59.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000159)  
and not contributing to the policies of the Dangerous Drugs Board.

**[00:25:04.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000160)  
We can contribute po. We can contribute po, Madam Chair, but we cannot sit as a regular member of the board, as an independent body.

**[00:25:13.680] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000161)  
So, yan po yung mandato sa atin ng konstitusyon.

**[00:25:17.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000162)  
Opo. Naiintindihan po natin yun, pero parte po din ng trabaho.

**[00:25:22.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000163)  
Human rights ng CHR ang pagprotekta sa mga vulnerable grupo, katulad ng mga kabataan,

**[00:25:29.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000164)  
and especially women, because the CHR has a gender—is the gender ombud.

**[00:25:35.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000165)  
Now, these people, yung mga youth, yung mga women, sila usually ang nabibiktima.

**[00:25:41.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000166)  
Ang karapatan nila ay nayuyurakan, natatapakan.

**[00:25:44.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000167)  
So, tungkulin ng CHR, then na maprotektahan sila.

**[00:25:47.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000168)  
So, how do we reconcile now this mandate and this duty?

**[00:25:52.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000169)  
If you are saying na hindi yan mandato po ng CHR?

**[00:25:56.640] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000170)  
Madam Chair, kung Montes, I'm with you sa karapatan po ng lahat ng ating mga vulnerable sectors,

**[00:26:03.600] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000171)  
but syempre po, CHR is not a part of the executive, but we can monitor and we are the advisor of the government.

**[00:26:13.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000172)  
Okay, thank you so much, Madam Chair, distinguished sponsor.

**[00:26:18.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000173)  
Ang sa akin lang po, it's also a matter of...

**[00:26:22.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000174)  
you know, balancing security and rights in policymaking.

**[00:26:26.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000175)  
If the CHR does not see it that way,

**[00:26:30.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000176)  
then perhaps there are other ways for which CHR can be able to contribute to policies

**[00:26:36.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000177)  
that affect human rights in the issue of drugs.

**[00:26:42.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000178)  
Madam Chair, Madam Speaker,

**[00:26:45.680] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000179)  
CHR seats as observed in all interagency bodies,

**[00:26:50.740] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000180)  
but it is also an activity...

**[00:26:52.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000181)  
It is also an activity for which CHR can participate in awarding recommendations to different agencies.

**[00:26:57.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000182)  
If that being so, Mr. Chair, kung nakaupo pala talaga doon,

**[00:27:01.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000183)  
ano po ang naging contribution ng CHR noong mga panahon na may mga aligasyon ng paglabag ng karapatang pantao

**[00:27:09.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000184)  
sa mga tokhang, sa mga EJ case, and all,

**[00:27:16.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000185)  
na naging sanhi kung bakit napasama yung ating iman,

**[00:27:22.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000186)  
kahit sa international community.

**[00:27:31.780] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000187)  
We are having our undertaking its own investigations.

**[00:27:36.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000188)  
So lahat po ng cases na nagkakaroon po tayo being forwarded po sa CHR,

**[00:27:41.880] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000189)  
unang-una po tinatanggap po natin yan.

**[00:27:44.340] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000190)  
Nagkakaroon po tayo ng investigation,

**[00:27:46.780] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000191)  
at syempre po, hanggang doon lamang po ang ating mandato,

**[00:27:49.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000192)  
we cannot file cases.

**[00:27:52.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000193)  
Pero we can endorse, recommend sa mga different agencies po.

**[00:27:57.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000194)  
Okay. Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair, distinguished sponsor.

**[00:28:03.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000195)  
Now let me move on.

**[00:28:06.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000196)  
I was just exploring the possibility based on the pronouncements of Congressman Teodoro

**[00:28:13.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000197)  
during the budget deliberation this morning.

**[00:28:16.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000198)  
So let me move on to another topic.

**[00:28:20.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000199)  
Recently, the CHR published...

**[00:28:22.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000200)  
I would like to emphasize that it provides free information, education, and campaign or IEC materials

**[00:28:27.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000201)  
to individuals and organizations to promote human rights,

**[00:28:31.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000202)  
particularly among vulnerable, marginalized, and disadvantaged sectors.

**[00:28:35.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000203)  
May I ask the CHR, does the Commission specifically maintain IEC materials

**[00:28:40.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000204)  
on the protection of women and violence, discrimination, harassment,

**[00:28:45.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000205)  
and other violations of human rights?

**[00:28:47.820] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000206)  
Madam Speaker, yes po.

**[00:28:50.260] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000207)  
Dire-direcho po ang ating...

**[00:28:52.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000208)  
IEC materials information sa ating pong komisyon.

**[00:28:57.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000209)  
Opo. Itong mga materials na ito, where do they exist?

**[00:29:02.740] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000210)  
Online po ba?

**[00:29:04.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000211)  
O are they distributed in far flung barangays, digital materials po lang ito?

**[00:29:11.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000212)  
Because sa pagkakaalam ko po, parang online na, no?

**[00:29:15.320] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000213)  
So, both physical and online po.

**[00:29:18.620] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000214)  
Available po yan.

**[00:29:20.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000215)  
So may mga distribution din.

**[00:29:22.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000216)  
Channels tayo.

**[00:29:22.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000217)  
How do we distribute it so that...

**[00:29:24.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000218)  
Yes po. Nagkakaroon po tayong...

**[00:29:26.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000219)  
Actually po, meron pong mga visitations sa various organizations,

**[00:29:30.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000220)  
sa ating pong mga barangays, municipality,

**[00:29:33.900] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000221)  
na umiikot po ang ating pong komisyon.

**[00:29:36.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000222)  
Opo. Opo.

**[00:29:38.980] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000223)  
How much po of your 2027 budget is allocated for the production,

**[00:29:44.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000224)  
printing, and dissemination of these materials?

**[00:29:48.320] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000225)  
Magkano po?

**[00:29:50.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000226)  
Ang...

**[00:29:51.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000227)  
Nakapaloob po.

**[00:29:52.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000228)  
Opo. Sa 2027 budget nila.

**[00:30:00.000] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000230)  
Madam Speaker, it's P94,643,000 ang aming proposed for 2027.

**[00:30:09.820] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000231)  
Out of the P94,000,000, magkano ang halaga specifically targeting women?

**[00:30:17.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000232)  
Out of the P94,000,000, ilan po doon ang halagang specifically nakatarget sa production of materials for women po?

**[00:30:27.900] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000233)  
For women?

**[00:30:30.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000234)  
Opo.

**[00:30:32.540] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000235)  
Naka-lump sum po siya, Madam Speaker.

**[00:30:35.940] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000236)  
So wala pong na-specific for women, for children; lump sum po siya para sa lahat po ng sectors.

**[00:30:43.940] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000237)  
Kasi nga po, Madam Speaker, medyo maliit po ang allocation po natin para sa buong Pilipinas po.

**[00:30:52.840] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000238)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[00:30:55.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000239)  
But I asked a while ago if there were materials specifically for,

**[00:30:59.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000240)  
addressing violence against women, and meron po.

**[00:31:03.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000241)  
So ibig sabihin po, ang nilalabas niyong materials, general na po yun.

**[00:31:07.720] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000242)  
Walang specific IEC material na para lang sa, that addresses gender violence.

**[00:31:14.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000243)  
Meron po.

**[00:31:14.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000244)  
Meron po.

**[00:31:16.080] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000245)  
So out of this yun po sana ang gusto kong itanong, kung magkano, and ano na lang po siguro, doon sa allocation na yun,

**[00:31:25.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000246)  
ano po ang target natin na maabot ng mga kababaihan?

**[00:31:29.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000247)  
Half of our population, 50% is women, ano po.

**[00:31:34.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000248)  
So ano po, maliit po ang budget ninyo, paano po natin maaabot o masasaklaw ang karamihan ng ating mga kababaihan?

**[00:31:42.860] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000249)  
Lalo na po yung mga nasa kanayunan.

**[00:31:46.220] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000250)  
Madam Speaker, ang aming pong GAD budget for 2027 ay 77 million pesos.

**[00:31:56.240] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000251)  
And doon po yun napupunta?

**[00:31:58.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000252)  
Yes po.

**[00:31:59.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000253)  
Okay po.

**[00:31:59.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000254)  
Maraming maraming salamat po.

**[00:32:03.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000255)  
Anyway, I still have a lot of questions but I committed to squeezing everything in.

**[00:32:12.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000256)  
So siguro, pang huli na lamang po, Madam Chair, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[00:32:18.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000257)  
nais ko na lamang pong ipahayag na ang kababaihan, kabalikatan para sa kapakanan at kaunlaran ng bayan o 4K

**[00:32:28.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000258)  
ay kaisa ng human rights.

**[00:32:29.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000259)  
Commission on Human Rights na ipagtangkol at isulong ang karapatang pantao sa harap ng malaking hamon na tugunan ang mga problema ng human rights

**[00:32:39.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000260)  
sa ating bansa, nais din nating hikayatin ng CHR na palawakin ang saklaw ng kanilang trabaho, patibayin ang pagganap ng tungkulin

**[00:32:49.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000261)  
at tiyakin na ang bawat pisong ipinagkakaloob ng taong bayan ay tinutumbasan ng pananagutan at kongkretong resulta.

**[00:32:57.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000262)  
Sa ganitong paraan,

**[00:32:59.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000263)  
maipapakita natin na ang pondo ay tunay na giging proteksyon at katarungan para sa mamamayan.

**[00:33:06.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-000264)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair, Distinguished Sponsor. Magandang gabi po sa inyong lahat.

**[00:33:12.840] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000265)  
[unclear] Leader.

**[00:33:24.040] Rep. Manuel "Wowo" L. Fortes Jr.** (asr-000266)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize Representative Antonio L.

**[00:33:29.340] Rep. Manuel "Wowo" L. Fortes Jr.** (asr-000267)  
Tinio of ACT Teacher Partylist.

**[00:33:32.840] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-000268)  
Representative Antonio Tinio of ACT Teacher Partylist is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[00:33:40.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000269)  
Salamat po, Madam Speaker.

**[00:33:43.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000270)  
Madam Speaker, ang tanong ko po ay kaugnay sa drug war, sa drug war ng nakaraang administrasyon ni dating Pangulong Duterte.

**[00:33:57.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000271)  
Yung extrajudicial killing.

**[00:33:59.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000272)  
At yung pagpaslang ng libu-libong mga kababayan nating Pilipino.

**[00:34:09.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000273)  
Isang malaking tagumpay po para sa naghahangad ng justisya, sa mga naghahangad ng justisya sa ating lipunan,

**[00:34:18.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000274)  
sa mga biktima ng extrajudicial killing sa kanilang mga pamilyang naiwan,

**[00:34:24.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000275)  
ang pagkaka-aresto.

**[00:34:29.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000276)  
At paglilitis kay dating Pangulong Duterte sa International Criminal Court.

**[00:34:38.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000277)  
So mahalagang tagumpay po yun.

**[00:34:41.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000278)  
At alam natin, hindi natatapos ang pagpapanagot kay Pangulong Duterte.

**[00:34:50.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000279)  
Bagaman siya ang, tama lang, siya bilang Pangulo, siya ang pinakamalaking isda.

**[00:34:57.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000280)  
Sa...

**[00:34:59.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000281)  
nag-anap na crimes against humanity.

**[00:35:05.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000282)  
Alam din natin na yung ICC nag-issue na ng warrant laban sa kanyang kanang kamay.

**[00:35:14.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000283)  
Yung dati niyang PNP chief naging senador, ngayon ay nagtatago sa warrant,

**[00:35:20.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000284)  
Sen. Bato de la Rosa, at sinasabi na mayroon pang ibang mga warrant na may issue.

**[00:35:28.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000285)  
Pero alam din natin,

**[00:35:29.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000286)  
na yung accountability para sa mga pagpaslang na iyan ay hindi limitado sa mga nasa pinakataas.

**[00:35:41.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000287)  
Yung chain of command at yung dugo ng extrajudicial killings ay mula sa taas,

**[00:35:52.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000288)  
dadalo yan sa hanggang sa panggitna, sa antas na panggitna hanggang sa pinakababa.

**[00:35:58.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000289)  
No?

**[00:35:59.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000290)  
Sa mga aktual na mga opisyal at rank and file ng PNP, among others,

**[00:36:06.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000291)  
na aktual na nagpatupad,

**[00:36:08.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000292)  
nung mga inutos sa taas,

**[00:36:10.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000293)  
yung aktual na mga pumatay.

**[00:36:12.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000294)  
So kaya po napakahalaga na sa loob ng ating bansa at sa pangunguna ng ating mga institusyon

**[00:36:25.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000295)  
ay magpatuloy ang trabaho na...

**[00:36:29.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000296)  
ng pag-iimbestiga at pagpapanagot sa lahat ng naging sangkot sa extrajudicial killings

**[00:36:37.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000297)  
sa ngalan ng peking drug war na ito.

**[00:36:40.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000298)  
Okay.

**[00:36:40.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000299)  
Kaugnay nun ng ang isa sa mga institusyon na inaasahan natin na magsagawa at magtuloy

**[00:36:50.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000300)  
sa napakahalagang gawaing ito ay ang Commission on Human Rights.

**[00:36:58.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000301)  
No? At...

**[00:36:59.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000302)  
Last year ay nagpahayag ang Commission on Human Rights na nagbukas sila ng imbestigasyon,

**[00:37:12.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000303)  
review and investigation of several hundred mga cases of drug war killings.

**[00:37:23.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000304)  
Pwede po bang for the record ay mabanggit lang

**[00:37:27.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000305)  
ilan po yung mga...

**[00:37:29.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000308)  
kabuo ang kaso na sinimulang investigahan muli ng Commission on Human Rights?

**[00:37:40.740] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000309)  
Madam Speaker, in particular, the Commission has identified 493 cases for reinvestigation in 2024

**[00:37:50.400] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000310)  
and the task force has directed of 289 cases reinvestigated in 2025.

**[00:37:58.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000311)  
Of the...

**[00:37:59.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000312)  
Of these 289 reinvestigated cases, 140 cases are currently for disposition by the task force.

**[00:38:08.140] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000313)  
The task force has also successfully reviewed and evaluated these cases identified for reinvestigation

**[00:38:15.420] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000314)  
and has begun drafting the corresponding resolution for disposition.

**[00:38:20.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000315)  
So, this process remains ongoing with the Commission committed to completing the disposition

**[00:38:29.040] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000316)  
of these cases by the end of this year.

**[00:38:32.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000317)  
Ayun.

**[00:38:32.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000318)  
So, yun lang po ang gusto natin ay makuha yung update.

**[00:38:37.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000319)  
So, for 2024, nabanggit niyo po ilang kaso?

**[00:38:42.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000320)  
493 cases.

**[00:38:44.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000321)  
493 cases.

**[00:38:45.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000322)  
Tapos for 20...

**[00:38:48.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000323)  
25?

**[00:38:49.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000324)  
25.

**[00:38:50.480] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000325)  
289 cases.

**[00:38:52.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000326)  
289 cases.

**[00:38:53.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000327)  
At sa 289 cases na yan, 140 po.

**[00:38:58.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000328)  
Tama po ba?

**[00:38:59.040] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000329)  
120 cases was transmitted and had undergone preliminary evaluation by the EGK task force.

**[00:39:07.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000330)  
Ah, 120 cases.

**[00:39:10.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000331)  
And then, yung for resolution na po, pending resolution ay ilan po?

**[00:39:17.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000332)  
Ito na po yun.

**[00:39:19.680] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000333)  
Ito na po yun.

**[00:39:20.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000334)  
Okay.

**[00:39:22.020] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000335)

**[00:46:33.940] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000422)

**[01:14:22.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000681)

**[01:14:30.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000683)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, meron lamang akong ilang katanungan related to the disposition of cases handled by the Commission.

**[01:14:38.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000684)  
Distinguished Sponsor, under the 2027 NEP ng CHR, can you describe what the Human Rights Protection Program is about?

**[01:14:48.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000685)  
And can the good sponsor also please describe how this program and its services can help an ordinary citizen, for example, a Moro or indigenous person? Thank you.

**[01:15:07.700] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000686)  
Madam Speaker, the Human Rights Protection Service is the operational arm responsible for investigating complaints,

**[01:15:17.160] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000687)  
manages cases, and delivering direct intervention for victims of human rights violations.

**[01:15:22.600] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000688)  
So, yung key functions and services po are independent investigations, quick response operations, legal aid and counseling, forensic and medical legal services, financial assistance, and witness protection.

**[01:15:43.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000689)  
Paano po kaya ito makakatulong sa isang ordinaryong mamamayan or ordinary citizen, for example, a moro or indigenous person?

**[01:15:52.600] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000690)  
Madam Speaker, yung ating mga clients, actually po, pwede po silang pumunta walk-in, physical in-person, o pwede rin po nilang bisitahin ang ating mga regional offices.

**[01:16:05.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000691)  
Meron po tayong 17 regional offices sa buong Pilipinas.

**[01:16:11.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000692)  
Pwede rin po silang mag-message sa FB.

**[01:16:13.940] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000693)  
Meron din po tayong online portal, yung tinatawag po natin mismo.

**[01:16:18.080] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000694)  
Meron din po tayong hotlines, emails.

**[01:16:20.440] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000695)  
Pwede rin po silang magbigay ng letters.

**[01:16:22.600] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000696)  
At saka motu proprio, our own investigation and initiative.

**[01:16:29.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000697)  
So, Madam Sponsor, if an ordinary citizen wants to avail the services of this Human Rights Protection Program, ano ang process for them to avail?

**[01:16:40.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000698)  
Bukod doon sa nabanggit ninyong mag-walk-in, magsusulat, paano po kaya yung proseso nila?

**[01:16:51.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000701)  
Sabihin natin, nag-walk-in po ako.

**[01:16:53.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000702)  
Pagdating ko doon, paano po kaya yung magiging proseso?

**[01:16:57.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000703)  
Wala po yung, ang komisyon po ay, wala po silang formal process na tinatawag.

**[01:17:04.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000704)  
Formal complaint.

**[01:17:05.560] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000705)  
Formal complaint. Lahat po ay, pag pumunta po doon, yung po ay agad-agad nating tutugunan.

**[01:17:13.080] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000706)  
Hindi po kailangan magkaroon ng formal complaint na dadalhin sa komisyon ang ating mga clients.

**[01:17:20.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000707)  
At tama po ba, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor,

**[01:17:22.600] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000708)  
wala itong timeline?

**[01:17:25.000] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000709)  
Wala po.

**[01:17:26.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000710)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:17:27.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000711)  
Given this, sa 2025 GAA, ang target ninyo sa pag-resolve ng human rights violation cases ay 90%.

**[01:17:36.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000712)  
Pero ang actual accomplishment ay 13.90% lang.

**[01:17:41.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000713)  
For cases finally disposed, kumbaga naisara na, within the prescribed period, ang target ay 95%, pero ang actual ay 53.30%.

**[01:17:52.600] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000714)  
Maari po bang ipaliwanag kung bakit hindi na-meet ang targets na ito?

**[01:18:04.560] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000715)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. Yes, we do confirm na 90% po ng ating targets sa pag-resolve ng cases with access to remedies,

**[01:18:14.980] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000716)  
lumalabas po na tila mababa ang accomplishment at 13.9%.

**[01:18:20.240] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000717)  
Ngunit ito po ay dahil.

**[01:18:22.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000718)  
Isinama namin sa bilang ang mga backlog cases mula pa po noong 1987 hanggang 2024.

**[01:18:29.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000719)  
Kapag tinignan po lamang ang new o yung pong 2025 na mga bagong kaso, ang ating pong accomplishment ay 86.67%.

**[01:18:39.880] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000720)  
Kaya nais po naming ipaliwanag na tila mababa po yung 53.30% dahil karamihan sa mga kasong naitala sa panahon ay natanggap po sa huling quarter

**[01:18:50.940] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000721)  
o mga buwan ng 2025. Kaya ito po

**[01:18:54.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000722)  
ay tumatakbo pa for investigation sa ating pong mga regional offices at siyempre po hindi naman po ito madali.

**[01:19:02.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000723)  
Kaya nahihirapan din po tayo.

**[01:19:04.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000724)  
Pero para ipaliwanag po ito sa mga region, kami po ay maraming investigation.

**[01:19:09.520] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000725)  
Marami po tayong kaso for investigation na mas madaming walk-in clients.

**[01:19:14.400] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000726)  
Kaya po yung kakulangan po ng personnel natin, isang factor pa rin po ito.

**[01:19:20.320] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000727)  
At nagkakaroon po tayo ng...

**[01:19:22.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000728)

**[01:19:26.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000729)  
Maraming salamat Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.

**[01:19:29.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000730)  
So matotal po ang lumalabas na kaya siya medyo mababa tignan ay dahil may mga nasa huling quarter na ito na pumasok ang cases

**[01:19:40.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000731)  
at kaya hindi pa naibibilang ang accomplishment niya.

**[01:19:44.820] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000732)  
Tama po, Madam Speaker.

**[01:19:46.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000733)  
At bilang isa din sa mga rason ay ang syempre ang kaso ay taon...

**[01:19:52.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000734)  
Taon ang bilang bago mo ito ma-re-resolve.

**[01:19:55.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000735)

**[01:19:58.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000736)  
But anyway, finally, Distinguished Sponsor, ano po ang kongkretong hakbang na nakikita ng CHR para masigurong ma-improve ang performance

**[01:20:08.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000737)  
at maabot ang mga itinakdang targets sa susunod na taon?

**[01:20:12.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000738)  
Madam Speaker, to my colleague, napakaganda po ng tanong ninyo.

**[01:20:18.240] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000739)  
To address the gaps po in the resolution and disposition of cases,

**[01:20:22.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000740)  
the investigation function of the CHR should first be strengthened before the resolution, disposition of cases be made.

**[01:20:31.740] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000741)  
Thus, the following line items is the budget should be increased.

**[01:20:35.620] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000742)  
Unang-una po yung travel expenses po natin, supplies, representation, postage and training expenses.

**[01:20:44.100] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000743)

**[01:20:50.140] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000744)

**[01:20:52.200] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000745)

**[01:20:59.640] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000746)  
Kaya po naman, hindi lamang po sa personnel, pati po sa services, talagang nagkakaroon po tayo ng gaps and lapses.

**[01:21:07.160] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000747)  
Pero gayon pa man po, hanggang sa makakaya nga po ng komisyon, ay gagawin po natin ang mandato po sa atin ng konstitusyon.

**[01:21:14.540] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000748)

**[01:21:16.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000749)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, dadako naman po ako sa mga katanungan na nais ipahatid

**[01:21:22.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000750)  
ng ating colleague, our Senior Deputy Minority Leader, Representative Leila de Lima.

**[01:21:29.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000751)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, the Philippines ranking in the World Justice Project Rule of Law Index showed a slight improvement overall.

**[01:21:38.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000752)  
Moving from 99th out of 142 countries in 2024 to 97th out of 143 countries in 2025.

**[01:21:49.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000753)  
However, the country remained.

**[01:21:52.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000754)  
118th in fundamental rights while its civil justice ranking deteriorated from 98th to 106th despite an improvement in criminal justice from 124th to 119th.

**[01:22:08.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000755)  
Sa kabila ng slight improvement overall, nananatiling mababa ang performance natin sa fundamental rights. Ano po ang nakikitang pangunahing gaps ng CHR at ano ang mga hakbang nitong ginagawa para matugunan ang mga ito?

**[01:22:28.620] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000756)  
Madam Speaker, on national protection mechanism, this aim for a comprehensive and institutionalized national mechanism that integrates

**[01:22:38.060] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000757)  
prevention, protection, and response. It also enhances coordination across stakeholders, utilizes data-driven system for decision-making, and also ensures inclusivity through GAD principle.

**[01:22:53.240] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000758)  
Ang PKP framework po aims to address the gaps. For example, yung delayed response to incidents, weak interagency coordination, at yung ating limited integration of data system.

**[01:23:07.080] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000759)  
Meron din po.

**[01:23:08.060] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000760)  
Mayroon din po tayong uneven access to service across regions by establishing a unified human rights.

**[01:23:16.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000761)  
Para ba tugunan ang pagbaba ng civil justice? Anong mga interventions ang kailangan ng CHR at may sapat po bang pondo or funds sa kasulukuyang budget para maisagawa ang mga ito?

**[01:23:30.080] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000762)  
Actually po, lagi po naman tayong hindi sapat ang pondo, Madam Speaker.

**[01:23:35.700] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000763)  
Pero siyempre po, we have the continuing

**[01:23:38.060] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000764)  
advocacy for passage of HRD laws and also lahat po ng bill for CADB.

**[01:23:48.380] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000765)  
Ang budget po kasi natin for 2027 para sa protection ay 95 million ay hindi po sapat para mapalawak at gumawa ng bagong mga programa at tustusan ang gastos ng 17 regional offices na nangangailangan po ng mga sasakyan,

**[01:24:08.060] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000766)  
gas at kung ano-ano pa para po sa ating mga imbestigasyon.

**[01:24:14.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000767)  
At para po sa huling katanungan, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, sa criminal justice naman, umangat tayo from 124 to 119.

**[01:24:25.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000768)  
Pero mababa pa rin ang standing natin.

**[01:24:27.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000769)  
Ano po ang specific programs ng CHR na nangangailangan ng additional resources upang mapalakas ang access to justice?

**[01:24:38.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000770)  
At human rights protection?

**[01:24:40.880] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000771)  
Unang-una po, Madam Speaker, kailangan po natin madagdagan ang budget ng ating komisyon para ma-establish po at mapalakas ang national protection mechanism para po sa ating human rights defenders.

**[01:24:54.920] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-000772)  
Katulad po ng ating bantay karapatang pantao, kalinga karapatang pantao, at tugon karapatang pantao, nagkakahalaga po itong an additional 13 million pesos.

**[01:25:08.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000773)  
Sa huli, ito'y lumalabas na nasa budget pa rin ang problema.

**[01:25:13.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000774)  
In closing, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor, I would like to manifest that the budget of the Commission on Human Rights should be sufficient to allow the CHR to fully carry out its constitutional mandate, which is to protect and uphold the rights of our people.

**[01:25:30.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000775)  
We trust that the guidance of the good sponsor, this chamber, will give due consideration to strengthening the CHR's budget.

**[01:25:40.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000777)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker, Madam Sponsor.
