**[01:44:09.700] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-001209)  
Session is suspended.

**[01:50:15.030] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-001222)  
Session is resumed. Majority Leader.

**[01:50:19.140] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001223)  
Madam Speaker, I move to consider the proposed budget of the Department of Information and Communications Technology and its attached agencies.

**[01:50:35.240] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001224)  
And for the purpose, Madam Speaker, may we acknowledge the heads and officials of the said department, namely Secretary Henry Rhoel Aguda; Chief of Staff Maria Teresa Magno Garcia; Undersecretary Heherson Asiddao.

**[01:51:03.760] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001225)  
Undersecretary Sarah Maria Sison; Undersecretary Leandro Angelo Aguirre; Undersecretary David Almirol Jr.; Undersecretary Leonido Pulido III; Undersecretary Rodil Aniban.

**[01:51:33.720] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001226)  
Undersecretary Christian Mark Guingcangco; Assistant Secretary Melvyn Carlo Barroa; Assistant Secretary Ryan Martin [unclear]; Assistant Secretary Maria Victoria Castro; Assistant Secretary Celine Melanie Dee; Assistant Secretary Pinky Jimenez; and Assistant Secretary Emmy Lou Versoza-Delfin.

**[01:52:17.080] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001227)  
Likewise representing the Cybercrime Investigation and Coordinating Center, Executive Director Renato Paraiso; the National Privacy Commission, Privacy Commissioner and Chairman Johann Carlos Barcena; and the National Telecommunications Commission, Commissioner Ella Blanca Lopez.

**[01:52:49.280] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-001227-split-2)  
We acknowledge the presence of the heads and officials of DICT and its attached agencies. Welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[01:52:59.160] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-001228)  
And with regard to the motion of the Majority Leader, is there any objection? The Chair hears none. The motion is approved. Majority Leader.

**[01:53:10.120] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001229)  
I move, Madam Speaker, that we recognize Vice Chair Brian Poe, the vice chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations, to sponsor the proposed budget of the Department of Information and Communications Technology and its attached agencies.

**[01:53:38.080] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-001230)  
Is there any objection? The Chair hears none. Representative Vice Chair Brian Poe, [unclear]. Of course, the vice chairman of the Committee on Appropriations is hereby recognized to begin the sponsorship of the budget of DICT and its attached agencies. Vice Chair may now proceed with his sponsorship speech.

**[01:54:02.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001231)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker. I happily address my colleagues here in plenary. I rise to sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Department of Information and Communications Technology and its attached agencies. For fiscal year 2027, DICT and its attached agencies are proposing a total expenditure program of 19.49 billion pesos, an increase of 1.27 billion, or 7%, from fiscal year 2026.

**[01:54:38.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001232)  
A significant portion of this budget goes to ICT infrastructure. The ICT Systems and Information Infrastructure Program receives 3.85 billion, as well as 1.13 billion for the National Broadband Plan, up by 69.3%, and 949.5 million for the National Government Data Center, up 27%.

**[01:55:04.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001233)  
These investments are already producing results. The National Fiber Backbone has laid 3,026 kilometers of fiber; 59 of 136 backbone nodes have been activated. And the Philippine Digital Infrastructure Project has an estimated cost of 16.1 billion, with 2.16 billion programmed for fiscal year 2027.

**[01:55:32.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001234)  
For connectivity, DICT reported 29,091 active free Wi-Fi points across 11,839—sorry, I stand corrected—11,836 locations as of July 2026. The Free Public Internet Access Program has 5 billion pesos in automatic appropriations for fiscal year 2027.

**[01:56:00.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001235)  
Our digital government infrastructure is also operating at a significant scale. The eGov PH application has surpassed 61 million downloads

**[01:56:10.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001245)  
and 56 million verified accounts, while eGov DX has

**[01:56:19.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001255)  
processed more than 1.2 billion transactions and integrated over 1,300 government e-services.

**[01:56:30.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001264)  
Cybersecurity, the National Security Operations Center, has expanded its coverage from 10 government agencies in 2022 to 99 agencies as of August 2026, monitoring approximately 6.7 billion security alerts.

**[01:56:48.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001265)  
But as we support these investments, we must also look closely at implementation. DICT's fiscal year 2025 obligation rate was 61.7%, leaving approximately 9.16 billion in unused appropriations.

**[01:57:09.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001266)  
For the ICT Systems and Information Infrastructure Program, obligation was 43.5%. Disbursement was 22.6%.

**[01:57:18.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001267)  
These figures remind us that the measure of budget is not only what we appropriate, but what is actually delivered.

**[01:57:27.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001268)  
And while we recognize the progress made, we also recognize that there are many shortcomings still, not only in cybersecurity, but also in combating fake news and other areas of digital safety and governance.

**[01:57:41.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001269)  
These are challenges that will require continued work and scrutiny.

**[01:57:48.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001273)  
And while many may look at it as an opportunity to reduce funding for the agency, I see this as a challenge to increase funding where it is critically necessary.

**[01:57:57.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001274)  
These programs are of the utmost importance for the development of our country in this new age of innovation, AI, cybersecurity, and other threats.

**[01:58:10.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001275)  
So, Madam Speaker, with that, we are ready to take the questions of our distinguished colleagues, and I thank you.

**[01:58:16.360] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-001276)  
Majority Leader.

**[01:58:21.740] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001277)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Robert Nazal of BH Party-list to interpellate on the proposed budget.

**[01:58:38.640] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-001278)  
Representative Robert Nazal of BH Party-list is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[01:58:44.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001279)  
Good evening, Madam Chair.

**[01:58:46.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001280)  
Good evening to my distinguished colleague.

**[01:58:50.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001281)  
Good evening.

**[01:58:51.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001282)  
And good evening to the DICT.

**[01:58:54.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001283)  
On the mandate of DICT, distinguished colleague, Mr. Sponsor, Madam Chair, I want to start with DICT's mandate.

**[01:59:06.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001284)  
DICT is supposed to lead our national ICT agenda.

**[01:59:09.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001285)  
And under Republic Act 12254,

**[01:59:16.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001286)  
we have an even bigger responsibility for whole of government digitalization and interoperability.

**[01:59:26.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001287)  
Madam Chair, distinguished colleague, Mr. Secretary, are you actually fulfilling that mandate?

**[01:59:34.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001288)  
And how?

**[01:59:36.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001289)  
Because DICT should be more than Wi-Fi and connectivity.

**[01:59:40.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001290)  
You should also make sure that government uses technology efficiently.

**[01:59:46.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001291)  
For example, are we oversubscribed in cloud services?

**[01:59:52.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001292)  
Do you know how much cloud capacity government is paying?

**[01:59:57.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001293)  
Paying for versus actual average and peak?

**[02:00:00.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001294)  
What about internet services? Are we paying for more bandwidth than government actually uses?

**[02:00:11.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001295)  
During our previous hearing, you told us Republic Act 12254 could save government at least 30% by eliminating repetitive common systems and subscriptions.

**[02:00:24.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001296)  
Have you already identified these duplications? How much can government actually save? And when will we see those savings?

**[02:00:35.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001297)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker. Those are very valid questions from my colleague. Let's go through it one by one very quickly.

**[02:00:44.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001298)  
So yes, Madam Speaker, DICT is implementing its mandate beyond the connectivity and moving towards a whole of government.

**[02:00:54.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001299)  
And that is currently being implemented through the DICT and its attached agencies.

**[02:01:06.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001300)  
For fiscal year 2027 alone, the department targets integration of 40 agencies with the Citizen Frontline Delivery Services Platform,

**[02:01:16.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001301)  
30 agencies, each of which with HCMIS and IFMIS, and 30 agencies with OFMIS.

**[02:01:24.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001302)  
And that includes the Online Public Service Portal and Procurement Systems.

**[02:01:28.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001303)  
Regarding the concern on cloud and bandwidth and other common ICT requirements,

**[02:01:34.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001304)  
the objective of e-Government Act is precisely to give government greater visibility over what common requirements exist

**[02:01:42.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001305)  
and to avoid paying repeatedly for the same capabilities.

**[02:01:46.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001306)  
So the management mechanism that we have here, and its ability to validate across

**[02:01:54.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001307)  
450 national agencies for compliance, is established within the eGov portfolio.

**[02:01:59.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001308)  
But we should not complain or I mean we should not claim, sorry, rather, that DICT is oversubscribed in terms of its cloud or bandwidth

**[02:02:10.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001309)  
because the submissions presently available to DICT do not yet establish government-wide average and peak utilization

**[02:02:18.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001310)  
across or against total contracted capacity.

**[02:02:22.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001311)  
So with that, we can...

**[02:02:24.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001312)  
We can commit to Congress to establish that a baseline through the inventory, portfolio management,

**[02:02:29.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001313)  
and interoperability mechanisms are being implemented under RA 12254.

**[02:02:36.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001314)  
The 30% figure that my colleague brought up, Madam Speaker, is a very valid number to bring up

**[02:02:42.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001315)  
because we did make that commitment.

**[02:02:45.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001316)  
And the figure, therefore, should be treated at this stage as a potential cost avoidance estimate

**[02:02:52.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001317)  
from eliminating duplicative...

**[02:02:54.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001318)  
...common systems and subscriptions, not yet as realized savings.

**[02:03:00.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001319)  
So really, this is the target that we hope to hit.

**[02:03:04.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001320)  
And yes, as of the last committee hearing that we had, Madam Speaker,

**[02:03:09.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001321)  
I do remember that the DICT is actively identifying where those duplications exist

**[02:03:15.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001322)  
and we're progressively moving towards a consolidated system, common ICT investments,

**[02:03:22.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001323)  
as agencies transition.

**[02:03:24.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001324)  
So I believe that we're really targeting that by the end of this administration,

**[02:03:32.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001325)  
we have a more centralized EGov system.

**[02:03:37.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001326)  
I hope that answers my colleague's questions.

**[02:03:39.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001327)  
Thank you for that, Distinguished Sponsor, Madam Chair.

**[02:03:42.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001328)  
Do we already have a number on the target in terms of the 30% savings that we will generate?

**[02:03:49.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001329)  
Do we already have a figure?

**[02:03:52.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001330)  
Madam Speaker, may I clarify?

**[02:03:53.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001331)  
A number on how much?

**[02:03:54.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001332)  
How much has been saved already?

**[02:03:55.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001333)  
Yes.

**[02:03:56.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001334)  
Let me get back to you on that.

**[02:04:01.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001335)  
Madam Speaker, the DICT's claim is that they don't have a set number yet,

**[02:04:09.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001336)  
although they are studying it.

**[02:04:11.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001337)  
Is there a target number in terms of savings?

**[02:04:16.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001338)  
For this year or for fiscal year 2027?

**[02:04:19.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001339)  
For this year.

**[02:04:21.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001340)  
For this year.

**[02:04:21.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001341)  
And fiscal year 2027.

**[02:04:24.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001342)  
But that's for this.

**[02:04:35.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001343)  
But for this year, we'll allow it.

**[02:04:38.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001344)  
Okay.

**[02:04:39.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001345)  
Because this is a new law, a newly passed law,

**[02:04:43.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001346)  
the actual estimated savings are for fiscal year 2027,

**[02:04:47.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001347)  
and we're looking at, if I'm not mistaken, 15 billion pesos in estimated savings

**[02:04:53.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001348)  
by removing

**[02:04:53.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001349)  
By removing these duplications.

**[02:04:56.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001350)  
Are we also looking at using AI to monitor all these systems?

**[02:05:04.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001351)  
So that...

**[02:05:05.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001352)  
Madam Speaker, DICT can confirm that we are using AI.

**[02:05:12.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001353)  
Thank you for that, Distinguished Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[02:05:15.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001354)  
I'll go to my next question.

**[02:05:18.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001355)  
Madam Chair, Madam Speaker, during the previous hearing,

**[02:05:21.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001356)  
Distinguished Sponsor, I also raised that the DICT has a 9% obligation rate.

**[02:05:29.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001357)  
We are told now that this has improved to around 27%.

**[02:05:33.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001358)  
Is this correct?

**[02:05:36.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001359)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, I can confirm we've increased to 27%.

**[02:05:40.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001360)  
So, okay naman po na hindi na 9%.

**[02:05:43.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001361)  
27% na, pero I hope we shouldn't be happy with this, no?

**[02:05:50.440] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001362)  
This is still very low.

**[02:05:51.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001363)  
May I ask, why, what is stopping us from improving on the 27%?

**[02:05:59.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001364)  
Ano ba ang problema natin?

**[02:06:00.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001365)  
Madam Speaker, I completely agree with my colleague.

**[02:06:03.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001366)  
27% is not something to celebrate,

**[02:06:05.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001367)  
especially when there are so many other agencies that need funding.

**[02:06:09.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001368)  
But it is an improvement.

**[02:06:10.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001369)  
And I'll list down the things that are making it difficult.

**[02:06:15.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001370)  
One is procurement delays.

**[02:06:16.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001371)  
Two is the technical complexity of what DICT's management,

**[02:06:21.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001372)  
mandate, puts them in charge of.

**[02:06:24.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001373)  
Three is the timing of the release of funds.

**[02:06:27.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001374)  
Many of the releases were quite late this year for DICT.

**[02:06:30.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001375)  
And so, many of the funds are just now being released.

**[02:06:35.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001376)  
We also have a limited specialized manpower.

**[02:06:38.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001377)  
Unlike many other agencies, DICT requires a lot of specialized manpower, Madam Speaker.

**[02:06:45.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001378)  
And with that, we actually do need to increase the manpower of the agency

**[02:06:50.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001379)  
in order to...

**[02:06:51.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001380)  
...increase its absorptive capacity and the services it provides.

**[02:06:56.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001381)  
Lastly, PDIP had two unsuccessful biddings and had to restructure its major procurement package.

**[02:07:05.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001382)  
And all of that combined leads to 27%.

**[02:07:09.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001383)  
What obligation rate, Mr. Sponsor, are we looking at for December 31?

**[02:07:21.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001384)  
Madam Speaker, the agency is optimistically pointing out that we're looking at 90% completion by December.

**[02:07:38.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001385)  
So, are we saying that considering what you've mentioned, Madam Chair and Mr. Sponsor,

**[02:07:47.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001386)  
maaayos na po natin yung procurement issues,

**[02:07:50.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001387)  
all the funnels,

**[02:07:51.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001388)  
kung saan po tayo nagkaka-roadblock?

**[02:07:54.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001389)  
In terms of technical manpower,

**[02:07:57.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001390)  
we will have all the technical manpower prior to that?

**[02:08:03.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001391)  
Just to clarify the target,

**[02:08:06.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001392)  
as of today, we're at 35% already.

**[02:08:09.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001393)  
As of today.

**[02:08:11.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001394)  
And we are expecting how much? 90?

**[02:08:13.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001395)  
90%.

**[02:08:14.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001396)  
And if you think about it,

**[02:08:16.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001397)  
when we had the initial data to today,

**[02:08:18.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001398)  
we already moved up 9%.

**[02:08:21.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001399)  
More or less.

**[02:08:23.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001400)  
If we continue at this rate,

**[02:08:24.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001401)  
we could possibly hit our target of 90%.

**[02:08:27.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001402)  
One of the things that the agency had to deal with

**[02:08:30.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001403)  
was really just waiting for the download of funding.

**[02:08:34.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001404)  
And so, there was time to already streamline some of the processes

**[02:08:38.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001405)  
for the release of these funds and the activation on the ground.

**[02:08:42.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001406)  
So, what you're saying, Distinguished Sponsor, Madam Speaker,

**[02:08:46.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001407)  
is that maayos na po tayo in terms of the procurement process

**[02:08:50.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001408)  
and all the technical manpower.

**[02:08:51.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001409)  
But our main problem right now is the DBM.

**[02:08:56.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001410)  
And how long, and kailan natin natatanggap yung pera?

**[02:09:01.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001411)  
Is this correct?

**[02:09:10.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001412)  
The reason why is not necessarily DBM.

**[02:09:15.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001413)  
It turns out it's because the awarding of major projects

**[02:09:17.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001414)  
to the DICT

**[02:09:21.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001415)  
will take place within this quarter.

**[02:09:23.940] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001416)  
So, once those have been awarded already,

**[02:09:25.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001417)  
that would be immediately dispersed.

**[02:09:27.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001418)  
But that doesn't necessarily have anything to do with the DBM.

**[02:09:30.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001419)  
When were these projects supposed to be procured?

**[02:09:35.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001420)  
When did they start?

**[02:09:49.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001421)  
But when did it start?

**[02:09:51.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001422)  
At the start of the year

**[02:10:14.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001426)  
So, PDIP, which constitutes 4 billion pesos, Madam Speaker,

**[02:10:20.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001427)  
is a big chunk of it.

**[02:10:21.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001428)  
And the reason why it was delayed

**[02:10:22.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001429)  
was because earlier this year, dear colleague,

**[02:10:26.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001430)  
they actually had a failed bidding.

**[02:10:28.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001431)  
So, now it's proceeding, but it's proceeding in October.

**[02:10:32.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001432)  
So, that's why the 4 billion will be released quite later.

**[02:10:35.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001433)  
But this was a project procurement for 2026 or 2025?

**[02:10:40.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001434)  
It's a continuing from 2025 to 2026.

**[02:10:44.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001435)  
Okay.

**[02:10:45.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001436)  
Let me go to my next question.

**[02:10:48.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001437)  
Having said that,

**[02:10:50.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001438)  
DICT's

**[02:10:51.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001439)  
absorptive capacity remains a problem,

**[02:10:54.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001440)  
I also want to be fair to the agency.

**[02:10:58.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001441)  
Hindi lahat ng hindi nababayaran ay necessarily Absorptive Capacity problem.

**[02:11:04.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001442)  
DICT's September 15 submission says

**[02:11:07.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001443)  
that as of August 31,

**[02:11:10.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001444)  
you had approximately 1.29 billion in accounts payable.

**[02:11:16.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001445)  
Of that, approximately 1.049 billion pertains to

**[02:11:21.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001446)  
valid and duly supported obligations from 2024 and 2025

**[02:11:26.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001447)  
that are already due and demandable.

**[02:11:30.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001448)  
You also say additional NCA from DBM is needed to settle these obligations.

**[02:11:36.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001449)  
So, for this 1.049 billion,

**[02:11:40.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001450)  
is the problem now lack of cash authority from DBM

**[02:11:44.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001451)  
rather than lack of appropriation?

**[02:11:51.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001452)  
How much additional NCA do you need today to settle all valid, due, and demandable obligations?

**[02:12:07.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001454)  
Yes, I can confirm that there is a payable of 1.049 billion and this, actually, we do need an additional allocation.

**[02:12:20.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001455)  
In order to pay for.

**[02:12:24.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001456)  
And these are considered as milestone payments, Madam Speaker.

**[02:12:32.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001457)  
So, Madam Chair, Madam Speaker,

**[02:12:38.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001458)  
if these are already valid, duly supported, and due and demandable obligations,

**[02:12:43.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001459)  
and DICT submits the complete requirements,

**[02:12:46.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001460)  
ang assumption po natin dito is complete na yung requirements nito.

**[02:12:50.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001461)  
Everything is complete.

**[02:12:54.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001462)  
So, Madam Speaker, I can confirm that all the requirements needed to request for additional funding for NCA from DBM

**[02:13:01.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001463)  
is complete and it's actually subject for approval already.

**[02:13:06.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001464)  
So, what is the reason?

**[02:13:08.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001465)  
What are they saying is the reason why hindi pa ito nababayaran?

**[02:13:14.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001466)  
Bakit po?

**[02:13:15.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001467)  
What is the reason for the delay?

**[02:13:18.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001468)  
Why is there no NCA?

**[02:13:45.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001479)  
Could you pause my time, please?

**[02:13:48.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001480)  
Okay.

**[02:13:48.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001481)  
The documents were submitted first semester of this year—sorry, third quarter of this year, dear colleague.

**[02:13:57.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001482)  
So that probably explains why until now the release has not yet been approved.

**[02:14:02.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001483)  
So that's what I do not understand.

**[02:14:06.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001484)  
Bakit po inabot tayo ng third quarter of this year to complete the requirements for something that is due and demandable since 2024?

**[02:14:15.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001485)  
Can you make me understand?

**[02:14:34.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001489)  
Why? Why wasn't it submitted? We already knew that this was going to be a project.

**[02:14:45.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001492)  
So it turns out that

**[02:14:48.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001494)  
The reason why there was a delay in the submission of the necessary documents was they needed to compile the requirements from the suppliers, etc.

**[02:14:57.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001495)  
And they were only able to do so later this year.

**[02:15:00.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001496)  
So, the delay is a mix of agency as well as suppliers.

**[02:15:11.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001497)  
What I cannot understand is that the DICT should be the number one digital agency in our country.

**[02:15:21.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001498)  
Kayo po ang lead sa lahat.

**[02:15:24.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001499)  
So, dapat po, kung meron man madi-delay, hindi po dapat kayo.

**[02:15:28.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001500)  
Dear colleague, I completely understand where you're coming from.

**[02:15:32.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001501)  
If there's any agency that should move quickly on a digitalized procurement and documentation process, it should be the DICT.

**[02:15:41.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001502)  
So, I believe I can get a commitment from the agency that they will improve their procurement processes.

**[02:15:48.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001503)  
Saka po, you know, distinguished sponsor, you know how easy it is now to review everything. We have AI.

**[02:15:57.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001504)  
But I hope...

**[02:15:58.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001505)  
I know that the DICT is going to work towards better digitalization in terms of its bureaucracy.

**[02:16:05.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001506)  
Pero sana din pati yung suppliers ng DICT, dito tayo abot sa compatibility ng data.

**[02:16:13.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001507)  
So, sana yung mga suppliers din ng DICT are up to standard in terms of their digitization para wala ng bottleneck.

**[02:16:20.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001508)  
Yes, I agree.

**[02:16:21.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001509)  
But of course, my understanding is that if you look at the way the DICT is paying,

**[02:16:28.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001510)  
walang supplier na magsusurvive sa ganitong sitwasyon.

**[02:16:34.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001511)  
So, the suppliers are effectively front-loading everything.

**[02:16:41.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001512)  
Madam Speaker, I understand that this disincentivizes some of our suppliers and potential investors

**[02:16:50.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001513)  
if this is how our lead digital agency deals with its documentation and disbursement processes.

**[02:16:56.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001514)  
But rest assured,

**[02:16:58.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001515)  
Madam Speaker, within this year, this outstanding balance should be settled by the DICT.

**[02:17:11.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001516)  
Madam Speaker, if I may also note that 90% of cash downloaded by the DBM has already been utilized.

**[02:17:19.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001517)  
So, it's really just a question of whether or not the funding is downloaded.

**[02:17:24.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001518)  
Once it is, it's immediately utilized by the agency.

**[02:17:27.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001519)  
So, it's really a factor.

**[02:17:28.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001520)  
So, it's really a funding issue.

**[02:17:31.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001521)  
Yeah, I would say that a big chunk of it would be.

**[02:17:36.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001522)  
And I hope that this can be streamlined on the agency side.

**[02:17:41.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001523)  
So, let us now go to earmarked funds.

**[02:17:47.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001524)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[02:17:51.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001525)  
separate from the regular budget of DICT,

**[02:17:56.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001526)  
ano naman ang absorptive capacity?

**[02:17:58.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001527)  
ng DICT when it comes to earmarked funds?

**[02:18:06.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001528)  
Lagi kasi namin kayong pinaglalaban because digitalization needs money.

**[02:18:12.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001529)  
And I know that.

**[02:18:13.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001530)  
And if we should be spending on anything, it should be, we should prioritize the digital infrastructure.

**[02:18:21.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001531)  
Pero kung may malaking problema, yung earmarked balance naman at hindi ninyo nagagastos, ano ba ang problema?

**[02:18:28.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001532)  
In terms of the earmarked funds, Madam Speaker, we're talking about the free Wi-Fi.

**[02:18:37.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001533)  
And that's at 61% in terms of utilization.

**[02:18:44.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001534)  
So, that speaks to the absorptive capacity of the agency to be able to utilize the earmarked funds.

**[02:18:51.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001535)  
But yes, 61%.

**[02:18:52.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001536)  
And that is what I cannot reconcile, Distinguished Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[02:18:58.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001537)  
Twelve digital systems hosted on the E-Gov PH were temporarily disabled

**[02:19:05.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001538)  
because DICT could not pay for cloud server space.

**[02:19:10.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001539)  
Twelve government systems went down.

**[02:19:14.280] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001540)  
So, meron kayong pera.

**[02:19:16.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001541)  
Paano po nangyari ito?

**[02:19:17.980] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001542)  
Ano ba nangyari talaga?

**[02:19:19.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001543)  
Can you explain?

**[02:19:21.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001544)  
How much was unpaid?

**[02:19:24.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001545)  
To whom?

**[02:19:25.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001546)  
How long were the systems down?

**[02:19:26.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001547)  
Down.

**[02:19:27.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001548)  
And was this all...

**[02:19:28.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001549)  
It's also an NCA problem.

**[02:19:47.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001550)  
Yung time,

**[02:19:49.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001551)  
It went down because, Madam Speaker, dear colleague, we had a surge of 700% in terms of its use.

**[02:19:57.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001552)  
So, the systems weren't...

**[02:19:58.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001553)  
We weren't able to handle that much.

**[02:20:02.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001554)  
And therefore, the systems went down for a short period of time.

**[02:20:10.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001555)  
Madam Speaker, it was only down for five hours on day one and six hours on day two.

**[02:20:21.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001556)  
But this is definitely because no one was able to predict a 700% surge in the use.

**[02:20:29.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001557)  
Paano po ba nangyari yung surge?

**[02:20:33.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001558)  
I mean, during that day or the day before that or the month before, ilan na ba yung gumagamit?

**[02:20:50.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001559)  
So, was it only on that day na talagang nagkaroon ng surge?

**[02:20:55.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001560)  
And the capacity could not absorb it?

**[02:20:57.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001561)  
Madam Speaker, it...

**[02:20:58.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001562)  
It turns...

**[02:20:59.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001563)  
Mr. Speaker, it turns out that the cloud services ended up running out of credits because of the overuse.

**[02:21:10.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001564)  
And that's how we ended up with that surge.

**[02:21:14.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001565)  
Running out of credit.

**[02:21:16.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001566)  
So, that means, hindi po natin...

**[02:21:18.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001567)  
We didn't have enough cash or money allotted for the capacity.

**[02:21:25.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001568)  
We didn't, Madam Speaker...

**[02:21:28.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001569)  
We didn't have enough budget enough for the surge that basically no one within the agency predicted that there would be a 700% surge in the use.

**[02:21:38.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001570)  
And therefore, the cloud services that were contracted weren't enough.

**[02:21:42.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001571)  
And therefore, we needed to procure more in order to adapt to the capacity required.

**[02:21:49.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001572)  
So, that's where I'm puzzled, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[02:21:56.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001575)  
Nag-down ang 12 government systems dahil kulang tayo sa pambayad sa cloud.

**[02:22:04.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001576)  
But you have earmarked revenues.

**[02:22:08.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001577)  
Meron po kayong pera na dapat kayo mismo ay may access doon at madali niyong magamit.

**[02:22:15.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001578)  
And this is for free Wi-Fi and for our cloud services.

**[02:22:19.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001579)  
Yes.

**[02:22:20.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001580)  
Madam Speaker, regarding that concern, that's correct that there are earmarked funds.

**[02:22:25.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001581)  
I don't...

**[02:22:27.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001582)  
All of it?

**[02:22:31.290] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001582-split-2)  
Right.

**[02:22:34.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001583)  
So, it's not included in the earmarked funds as part of the automatic use.

**[02:22:41.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001584)  
So, for data, we're unable to use those earmarked funds in order to fund cloud data.

**[02:22:48.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001585)  
Are we expecting this to happen again?

**[02:22:51.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001586)  
Or do we have enough funds already and credits, so-called credits, to cover any surge in the future?

**[02:22:57.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001587)  
And do...

**[02:22:58.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001588)  
Do we have access to the earmarked funds para hindi na ito mangyari ulit?

**[02:23:02.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001589)  
So based on the eGov law, Madam Speaker, we do have enough funds to maintain.

**[02:23:11.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001590)  
But that doesn't necessarily mean that we are at optimal.

**[02:23:15.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001591)  
If I'm not mistaken, the budget that we're being given for these cloud services...

**[02:23:21.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001592)  
I mean, for our data services is about 949 million.

**[02:23:26.080] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001593)  
For the indulgence of...

**[02:23:27.880] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001595)  
For the honorable Nazal, if he could kindly wind up.

**[02:23:32.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001596)  
Right.

**[02:23:33.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001597)  
So, 949 million for eGov.

**[02:23:35.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001598)  
But the reality is, we actually need additional funds for redundancies.

**[02:23:41.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001599)  
We need additional funds for emergency systems, which we don't have currently.

**[02:23:48.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001600)  
So, if you're wondering why there's a risk of it going down,

**[02:23:51.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001601)  
it's partly because we're underfunding this specific program.

**[02:23:54.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001602)  
But the law does provide for the bare minimum required.

**[02:23:57.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001603)  
to operate and maintain eGov.

**[02:24:00.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001604)  
So, what is the funding actually needed for us to be able to support?

**[02:24:10.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001605)  
The request is 1.8 billion, Madam Speaker.

**[02:24:15.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001606)  
So, bakit pa po natin kunin ito sa USF?

**[02:24:20.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001607)  
Um...

**[02:24:22.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001608)  
Because...

**[02:24:24.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001609)  
...

**[02:24:27.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001610)  
Well, that's a very valid point that the USF could possibly be used,

**[02:24:36.100] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001611)  
or earmarked funds could possibly be used for cloud services.

**[02:24:39.860] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001612)  
However, unfortunately, that is not within the purview of the agency to decide that.

**[02:24:45.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001613)  
I believe that is a call of the DBM, if I'm not mistaken, Madam Speaker.

**[02:24:50.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001614)  
Okay.

**[02:24:53.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001615)  
Maybe due to lack of time, I just have to bring this up.

**[02:24:58.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001616)  
During our previous hearing also, we already discussed that much of the government data is hosted abroad.

**[02:25:03.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001617)  
And that DICT needs local availability zones or data centers.

**[02:25:08.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001618)  
Hindi ko na po ulitin yun.

**[02:25:09.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001619)  
Ang follow-up ko lang, kailan matatapos ang migration?

**[02:25:18.700] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001620)  
What percentage of critical government data and workloads will be locally hosted by end of November 26?

**[02:25:25.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001621)  
By end of November 2027?

**[02:25:27.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001622)  
And when will migration be substantially completed?

**[02:25:34.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001623)  
By the end of this year, we're looking at 20% of critical government data would be localized.

**[02:25:40.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001624)  
We currently have one server that is up in Subic.

**[02:25:46.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001625)  
One data center that's up in Subic.

**[02:25:48.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001626)  
And we're hoping that we'll be able to localize our government data all by 2028.

**[02:25:55.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001627)  
That's the goal, Madam Speaker.

**[02:25:57.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001628)  
And how much is needed to localize everything by 2028?

**[02:26:05.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001629)  
Based on the feasibility studies, DICT is projecting 8 billion in costs, Madam Speaker.

**[02:26:19.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001630)  
Madam Chair, Distinguished Sponsor, I go back to where I started.

**[02:26:25.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001631)  
DICT's mandate is much bigger than Wi-Fi.

**[02:26:27.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001636)  
connectivity, computers and subscriptions.

**[02:26:31.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001637)  
DICT is supposed to lead the digital transformation of government.

**[02:26:36.580] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001638)  
That means efficient ICT spending, reliable e-gov systems, interoperable databases,

**[02:26:42.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001639)  
trusted digital identity, transparent government processes,

**[02:26:47.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001640)  
and public spending that citizens can digitally trace.

**[02:26:52.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001641)  
Lagi naman natin pinaglalaban na mabigyan kayo ng sapat na resources ang DICT,

**[02:26:57.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-001642)  
Because digitalization is essential. But if you need more money, tell Congress exactly what you need and what it will deliver. And if the money is already available, particularly from earmarked resources, then we need to understand why it is not being converted into reliable government services. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:27:19.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001643)  
Madam Speaker, I want to thank our colleague for his constructive questions. They're very valid and they really point to the needs of the agency.

**[02:27:35.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001644)  
Majority Leader.

**[02:27:37.280] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001645)  
To further interpellate on the proposed budget, Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the representative of Kabataan Party-list,

**[02:27:49.840] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-001646)  
Representative Renee Louise Co.

**[02:27:54.160] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001647)  
Honorable Renee Louise Co of Kabataan Party-list is hereby recognized for her interpellation.

**[02:28:00.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001648)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. And good evening to our distinguished sponsor and to DICT.

**[02:28:07.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001649)  
So may this distinguished sponsor yield to a couple of questions regarding the budget of DICT?

**[02:28:18.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001650)  
Madam Speaker, it would be my privilege.

**[02:28:19.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001651)  
It would be my privilege to answer the questions of my dear colleague.

**[02:28:23.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001652)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[02:28:25.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001653)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, my first line of questioning would be in relation to the free public internet access program,

**[02:28:34.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001654)  
which this representation thanks the DICT for the report that it has provided,

**[02:28:40.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-001655)

**[02:51:51.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001992)

**[02:51:53.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001993)  
moving towards 2028, we need another 8 billion to reach our target.

**[02:51:59.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001994)

**[02:52:09.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001996)

**[02:52:20.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-001997)

**[02:52:24.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002000)

**[02:53:10.940] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002011)

**[02:53:35.230] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002012)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker. Madam Speaker, ah, yung mga questions natin regarding the DICT budget have to do with the

**[02:53:53.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002014)  
the project.

**[02:53:57.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002015)  
So, practically all the projects of the ICT, and these include the National ICT Household Survey,

**[02:54:10.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002016)  
the National Government Data Center Infrastructure, National Broadband Program,

**[02:54:16.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002017)  
the National Government Portal, the Government Transparency and Accountability Program,

**[02:54:23.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002018)  
they're all funded at the central office level.

**[02:54:33.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002019)  
So, lahat sila.

**[02:54:36.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002020)  
Unlike many other agencies na merong nasa central office, centrally managed, tas merong nasa regions.

**[02:54:44.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002021)  
So, lahat po konsentrado sa national.

**[02:54:49.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002022)  
In fact, parang yun na yung ano eh, yun na yung mga major programs.

**[02:54:52.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002023)  
Those are all.

**[02:54:53.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002024)  
Those are all the programs, in fact, if I'm not mistaken, of the DICT.

**[02:54:58.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002025)  
So, una, is there an explanation for that?

**[02:55:06.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002026)  
In other words, highly centralized.

**[02:55:09.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002027)  
Madam Speaker, for the longest time, for the past decade, the budget of the DICT has been centralized in the Secretary's office.

**[02:55:20.020] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002028)  
But that doesn't necessarily mean...

**[02:55:23.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002029)  
That the DICT agrees that this is how it should remain.

**[02:55:26.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002030)  
In fact, there are steps being taken between the DICT and DBM to push towards decentralization of the fund that's being given to DICT

**[02:55:36.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002031)  
so that we don't have 90% of the budget being allocated to the Secretary's office year after year.

**[02:55:43.020] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002032)  
As our ICT concerns evolve, and to make sure that the distribution of ICT services is more equitable,

**[02:55:50.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002033)  
the argument for decentralization becomes more and more...

**[02:55:53.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002034)  
the argument for decentralization becomes more and more clear.

**[02:55:55.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002035)  
Alright. So, but ano ba yung reason?

**[02:55:58.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002036)  
Is it historical? Is it because relatively bagong department ng DICT?

**[02:56:04.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002037)  
Ano pong dahilan?

**[02:56:07.400] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002038)  
So, yes, it's historical, but it's also because of the capacity of the regional offices.

**[02:56:13.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002039)  
Sa totoo lang po, medyo bata pa ang ahensya.

**[02:56:16.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002040)  
So, yung mga regional offices po natin need to get up to speed in terms of technical capability and manpower

**[02:56:23.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002044)  
able to address decentralization

**[02:56:25.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002045)  
from the Secretary's office.

**[02:56:27.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002046)  
Okay.

honed in on the

the proposed

just to highlight

there are the online social media

**[03:29:38.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003060)  
which is basically

**[03:29:40.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003061)

**[03:29:42.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003062)

**[03:29:44.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003063)  
my colleagues here from DICT

are saying that they have

**[03:29:48.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003065)  
roadshows and partnerships

**[03:29:50.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003066)  
with the LGUs

**[03:29:52.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003067)  
to push information

**[03:29:54.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003068)
