**[03:29:57.020] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003069)  
So, meron po,

**[03:29:58.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003070)  
kahit wala namang pondo,

**[03:30:00.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003077)  
naghahanap ng paraan po ang DICT para sa kanilang information campaigns to protect from online sexual abuse and misinformation and scams.

**[03:30:11.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003078)  
Maraming salamat. At Madam Speaker, natanong ko ito dahil kamakailan lang maraming lumabas na mga exclusive reports

**[03:30:20.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003079)  
tungkol sa mas matinding, mas laganap, mas marami ng mga kaso ng technology-facilitated gender-based violence.

**[03:30:28.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003080)  
Isa na yung exclusive report mula sa Rappler tungkol sa 430,000 plus member forum na nagsistream ng mga privado at intimate moments ng mga kababaihan.

**[03:30:44.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003081)  
Nandyan din po ang lumabas na ulat mula sa CNN tungkol sa emergence ng mga global rape academy.

**[03:30:55.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003082)  
Napakahirap pong sabihin kasi hindi po katanggap.

**[03:30:58.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003083)  
Nandyan din po ang mga espasyo, mga Philippine online forum na nagbibigay ng rewards sa submission ng mga non-consensual intimate content.

**[03:31:14.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003084)  
Sa mga platforms na namag-get yung kanina, sa ating hotline, sa ating online platforms,

**[03:31:21.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003085)  
yung ating po bang mga first responders, mga tumatanggap ng tawag,

**[03:31:28.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003086)  
sumailalim sa isang gender sensitivity training, first aid responders training para po sa pagtanggap ng ganitong tipo ng mga kaso na may kinalaman sa gender-based violence.

**[03:31:53.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003087)  
So legitimate naman talaga po yung concern ng colleague po natin.

**[03:31:58.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003088)  
Dapat may tamang training po ang ating first responders.

**[03:32:04.940] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003089)  
The CWC is in partnership with the CICC in providing our first responders with the proper training.

**[03:32:13.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003090)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[03:32:14.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003091)  
Bukod po sa Council for the Welfare of Children para sa pagtitiyak na child-friendly ang ating pag-usap,

**[03:32:22.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003092)  
sensitive sa mga needs ng ating mga bata at ng mga kabataan,

**[03:32:28.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003093)  
halimbawa sa Philippine Commission on Women,

**[03:32:30.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003094)  
yung partnership natin para sa pagtitiyak na hindi lang gender-sensitive pero gender-affirmative din.

**[03:32:39.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003095)  
Yung pagtitiyak natin na lahat na hindi lang naman po kababaihan,

**[03:32:44.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003096)  
pinakamarami pa rin sa mga kababaihan, pinakamadalas,

**[03:32:47.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003097)  
pero maaari din pong nagiging target ng abuse ang men, boys, at gender-diverse people.

**[03:32:56.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003098)  
Magandang suggestion.

**[03:32:58.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003099)  
Magandang suggestion po yan, Madam Speaker.

**[03:33:01.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003100)  
May commitment po ako galing sa CICC na agad-agad they will reach out to the Commission on Women

**[03:33:07.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003101)  
to be able to get their input as well regarding this matter.

**[03:33:11.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003102)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[03:33:13.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003103)  
At meron na tayong mga civil society organizations na nagsisimula na mag-develop ng modules

**[03:33:20.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003104)  
para po pakipagtulungan sa paglaban sa technology-facilitated gender-based violence.

**[03:33:25.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003105)  
At aasahan po natin.

**[03:33:27.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003106)  
Sana po for 2027, meron din mga pondo ang DICT, ang National Privacy Commission

**[03:33:34.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003107)  
para mapagyaman ang mga tulungan na ito at lahat tayo ay magsama-sama sa paglaban sa TFGBV.

**[03:33:44.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003108)  
Ngayon naman po, I'd like to ask about the role of the DICT as well as the National Privacy Commission

**[03:33:52.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003109)  
on the recent adoption of the International...

**[03:33:57.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003110)  
International Labor Organization Convention No. 193.

**[03:34:01.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003111)  
This is the very first international labor standard created specifically for digital platform workers

**[03:34:10.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003112)  
such as those working in the gig economy, delivery and online application workers.

**[03:34:16.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003113)  
This is particularly relevant for DICC, for example, because it covers the protection,

**[03:34:27.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003114)  
on algorithmic transparency and management, requiring online platforms to inform workers

**[03:34:36.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003115)  
and their representatives about automated monitoring and evaluation systems

**[03:34:41.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003116)  
and to provide safeguards against unlawful or discriminatory suspension, deactivation or termination.

**[03:34:49.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003117)  
May we know, Madam Speaker, if the DICC or the NPC already have their position on this?

**[03:34:57.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003118)  
And will the departments, will the commissions actively campaign for its ratification here in the Philippines,

**[03:35:05.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003119)  
considering that we have a considerable number of workforce working in the ICT-related sector?

**[03:35:13.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003120)  
And how does the ICT and the commissions here intend to consult their stakeholders

**[03:35:20.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003121)  
from the platform managers, administrators, owners, to the workers?

**[03:35:27.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003122)  
May kasama po ba tayong pondo para matiyak na broad, wide,

**[03:35:33.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003123)  
yung mga magiging konsultasyon natin sa stakeholders para sa ILO Convention No. 193?

**[03:35:57.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003124)  
Madam Speaker, nabanggit ko po dito ang NPC dahil isa din sa mga guarantees ng convention na ito,

**[03:36:11.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003125)  
ang data protection and privacy.

**[03:36:14.340] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003126)  
Unfortunately, Madam Speaker, sa ngayon wala pa silang policy regarding this matter

**[03:36:20.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003127)  
at wala po silang pondo to support furthering this.

**[03:36:26.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003128)  
However,

**[03:36:27.420] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003129)  
in terms of stakeholder consultation,

**[03:36:30.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003130)  
I have confirmation that they do have stakeholder consultations as well as capacity building.

**[03:36:36.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003131)  
But for this specific matter, wala pa po silang internal policy.

**[03:36:42.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003132)  
But maybe I can ask my colleague and our other colleagues in the minority

**[03:36:47.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003133)  
for their position regarding this to help inform the agency.

**[03:36:54.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003134)  
Madam Speaker, will the distinguished sponsor, will the department,

**[03:36:57.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003135)  
commit to a study this particular convention and sit not only with these representatives

**[03:37:04.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003136)  
but stakeholders from workers' groups who are also calling for algorithmic transparency?

**[03:37:12.980] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003137)  
There is a commitment from the DICT and its attached agency to conduct the necessary study for this.

**[03:37:20.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-003138)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker. Maraming salamat sa ating distinguished sponsor.

**[03:37:23.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003139)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[03:37:24.260] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003140)  
Majority Leader?

**[03:37:26.780] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-003142)  
Maraming salamat.

**[03:37:27.380] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-003143)  
Madam Speaker, last to interpellate on the proposed budget is Philreka Party-list representative.

**[03:37:40.060] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-003144)  
I therefore move that we recognize Congressman Presley De Jesus.

**[03:37:46.600] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003145)  
The distinguished gentleman representing Philreka Party-list,

**[03:37:50.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003146)  
Honorable Presley De Jesus, is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[03:37:57.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003148)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker, and to the distinguished sponsor.

**[03:38:03.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003149)  
After the Ateneo de Zamboanga school shooting,

**[03:38:08.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003150)  
DICT and CICC publicly raised the possibility of blocking Facebook as a last resort

**[03:38:17.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003151)  
over delayed takedowns of violent content, child safety concerns, and misinformation.

**[03:38:24.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003152)  
Malakanyang later said there is no ban for now,

**[03:38:27.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003153)  
and that the government is cooperating.

**[03:38:28.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003154)  
And talks were held in Singapore.

**[03:38:32.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003155)  
What really transpired in Singapore?

**[03:38:35.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003156)  
Nagkaroon ko ba ng usapan po nung pumunta po kayo?

**[03:38:39.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003157)  
Because I remember during committee hearing, Madam Chair,

**[03:38:42.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003158)  
nabanggit na [unclear] Secretary, nagkaroon daw ng dialogue sa Singapore.

**[03:38:47.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003159)  
Can you update us what transpired on that said meeting?

**[03:38:51.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003160)  
Yes. Madam Secretary, if I'm not mistaken, members of the DICT and CICC,

**[03:38:57.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003161)  
flew to Singapore to consult and explain the situation here in the Philippines to META.

**[03:39:05.580] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003162)  
And I believe they're working towards an MOU, an MOA, rather, with META

**[03:39:13.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003163)  
to be able to address the growing concerns of influence on minors here in the Philippines,

**[03:39:19.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003164)  
among other concerns that are happening within the META platform, Madam Speaker.

**[03:39:24.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003165)  
Thank you.

**[03:39:27.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003166)  
Madam Speaker, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[03:39:32.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003167)  
one of the primary mandates of the DICT is the security of critical ICT infrastructure,

**[03:39:41.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003168)  
which includes the information assets of government, individuals, and businesses,

**[03:39:47.460] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003169)  
as well as the protection of consumers' privacy, security, and confidentiality.

**[03:39:53.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003170)  
My question will focus on DICT's follow-through,

**[03:39:57.380] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003171)  
as well as the security of the META platform.

**[03:39:58.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003172)  
The DICT also announced an agreement with META on online scams,

**[03:39:58.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003173)  
particularly those occurring through Facebook and other META platforms.

**[03:40:05.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003174)  
The DICT announced an agreement with META as early as February 2026,

**[03:40:12.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003175)  
covering fake investment schemes, phishing, digital fraud, online scams, and deep fakes.

**[03:40:21.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003176)  
What does the agreement actually entail, Mr. Speaker?

**[03:40:27.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003177)  
So, Madam Speaker, the agreement that was being referred to is separate from the MOA that they're currently working on.

**[03:40:37.020] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003178)  
And to update my colleague, because of the urgency,

**[03:40:41.140] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003179)  
as of yesterday, the DICT and CICC have already started their technical working group with META

**[03:40:47.900] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003180)  
to be able to address the concerns of our constituents with regards to these matters.

**[03:40:53.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003181)  
However, on the status of the TWG,

**[03:40:57.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003182)  
because they're just about to start,

**[03:40:59.280] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003183)  
they're only doing role assignments and immediate target setting.

**[03:41:04.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003184)  
But as the days go on, we'll see immediate action from the relevant agencies, Madam Speaker.

**[03:41:12.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003185)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[03:41:14.040] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003186)  
By the way, how many, as far as Facebook account,

**[03:41:19.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003187)  
do they have Facebook or the META as an office here in the Philippines?

**[03:41:27.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003188)  
Madam Speaker, I would like to confirm that Facebook does have a physical office here in the Philippines.

**[03:41:36.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003189)  
However, we don't have jurisdiction over them, Madam Speaker,

**[03:41:39.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003190)  
dahil marketing office lang po ito.

**[03:41:41.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003191)  
So, in short, Madam Speaker, wala talaga silang office, only marketing arm.

**[03:41:47.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003192)  
Ibig sabihin, pang-marketing, pang-recruit lang.

**[03:41:51.480] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003193)  
So, what I'm trying to say, do they have a country manager here in the Philippines?

**[03:41:57.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003195)  
And how many employees do they employ?

**[03:42:02.120] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003196)  
There's only a handful of employees here in the Philippines, Madam Speaker,

**[03:42:05.780] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003197)  
and they don't have an official country manager for the Philippines.

**[03:42:09.320] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003198)  
But I would like to assure my colleague that one of the terms that,

**[03:42:13.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003199)  
the two of the terms that are being considered in this technical working group

**[03:42:18.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003200)  
is one, that they do have a physical office here in the Philippines,

**[03:42:24.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003201)  
a corporate office here in the Philippines,

**[03:42:25.660] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003202)  
and that two,

**[03:42:27.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003203)  
that they require the national ID already for users of META here in the Philippines.

**[03:42:35.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003204)  
And it's been done before.

**[03:42:36.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003205)  
I want to point that out, that Brazil has already implemented such a system

**[03:42:41.480] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003206)  
because of their age restrictions.

**[03:42:44.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003207)  
So, it is possible.

**[03:42:44.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003208)  
Madam Speaker, what I'm trying to say, do they have office?

**[03:42:49.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003209)  
Do they have managers?

**[03:42:51.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003210)  
Not the marketing itself.

**[03:42:53.180] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003211)  
Yun ang gusto naming mangyari.

**[03:42:54.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003212)  
See?

**[03:42:54.660] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003213)  
There's none.

**[03:42:55.700] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003214)  
Madam Speaker, we don't have jurisdiction.

**[03:42:57.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003215)  
I agree with my colleague.

**[03:42:58.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003216)  
It's ridiculous.

**[03:42:59.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003217)  
Considering that the Philippines is the fifth largest population on META,

**[03:43:03.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003218)  
that META does not have an actual country manager and a real office here in the Philippines,

**[03:43:10.060] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003219)  
considering the amount of capital that this country provides META through its engagement on the platform.

**[03:43:17.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003220)  
So, I agree with my colleague here.

**[03:43:20.020] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003221)  
This is a priority for us.

**[03:43:21.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003222)  
Madam Speaker, the distinguished sponsor mentioned a while ago that we are,

**[03:43:26.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003223)  
the fifth largest users of META.

**[03:43:30.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003224)  
Imagine, the entire, sa buong mundo, pang lima tayo,

**[03:43:36.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003225)  
and we're only, what, 120 million Filipinos compared to other countries,

**[03:43:42.740] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003226)  
transing billions.

**[03:43:44.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003227)  
But again, so we're being ignored.

**[03:43:46.860] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003228)  
I'm not trying to blame the ICT, Madam Speaker.

**[03:43:50.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003229)  
Ito po yung sinasabi natin.

**[03:43:53.580] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003230)  
We keep on making their businesses.

**[03:43:56.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003231)  
Big.

**[03:43:57.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003232)  
But in return, hindi mo lang tayo mapansin.

**[03:44:01.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003233)  
Not even as office here in the Philippines, hindi maibigay sa atin.

**[03:44:07.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003234)  
And they have to follow the Philippine laws, Madam Speaker.

**[03:44:12.280] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003235)  
So, my next question, ano po ang ginawa ng DICT?

**[03:44:17.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003236)  
Did you persuade them na magtayo kayo sa opisina?

**[03:44:23.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003237)  
Opisina, that you have a country manager,

**[03:44:25.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003238)  
that you have the organizational structure that will look into sa mga scammers?

**[03:44:32.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003239)  
Ang dami nang lumalabas, kawawa ang ating mga kababayan.

**[03:44:36.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003240)  
Well, the two non-negotiables, Madam Speaker,

**[03:44:39.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003241)  
are one, that they open an actual office here in the Philippines

**[03:44:42.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003242)  
so that they can be held accountable.

**[03:44:44.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003243)  
And two, Madam Speaker, again, for the sake of accountability and protection on social media,

**[03:44:50.360] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003244)  
the requirement of national ID in order to create or maintain your account on the META platform,

**[03:44:55.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003245)  
here in the Philippines.

**[03:44:57.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003246)  
So, these are steps that are being taken.

**[03:45:00.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003247)  
To be fair to META, META seems open to these requirements and have even cited other countries in which they had similar requirements and acted on it.

**[03:45:10.820] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003248)  
So we're moving in that direction and the results of this technical working group that just started, hopefully we'll be able to see it within the next few months.

**[03:45:21.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003249)  
Again, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, the concern is that DICT has several programs, publicized partnerships and coordination mechanisms, but we know, but we don't have visibility of the result of those initiatives.

**[03:45:40.400] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003250)  
What system does DICT currently have to ensure that actions announced by the department are actually monitored through implementation?

**[03:45:51.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003251)  
Madam Speaker, I want to share with my colleague here and all the members of Congress that there is actual action that DICT is taking to hold META accountable.

**[03:46:03.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003252)  
And what I have here in my hand, Madam Speaker, is a copy of several documents that have been submitted to the Office of the Solicitor General to possibly build up a case against META for abuses that take place on their platform,

**[03:46:20.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003253)  
which they turn...

**[03:46:21.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003254)  
A blind eye too.

**[03:46:23.260] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003255)  
So in this, we have in Annex A, a draft of a civil complaint, which was prepared by the DICT.

**[03:46:31.020] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003256)  
In Annex B, there is a formal request drawing from global litigation precedents in Brazil, New Mexico, and Los Angeles that recognize platforms as active participants rather than passive intermediaries of actions taken on their platform.

**[03:46:48.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003257)  
Annex C actually has...

**[03:46:51.440] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003258)  
All the evidence and documentation of abuse, that's OSAEC, CSAEM, financial fraud, online debt harassment, illegal gambling,

**[03:47:01.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003259)  
records of platform knowledge and inaction is also included in this document in Annex C.

**[03:47:09.240] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003260)  
In Annex D, the National Privacy Commission asserted its regulatory jurisdiction over META

**[03:47:17.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003261)  
in relation to the processing of personal data for AI-generated content and functionalities.

**[03:47:25.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003262)  
And the NTC submitted its legal position paper on a test case for courts to propose action to be taken against Facebook in Annex E.

**[03:47:36.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003263)  
So the DICT and its attached agencies aren't just sitting around waiting for something to happen.

**[03:47:44.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003264)  
They've already prepared the necessary evidence, documents,

**[03:47:47.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003265)  
and potential cases to hold META accountable should they not work together with our government.

**[03:47:54.380] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003266)  
So I would like to request the assistance of a page to pass a copy of this to my colleague

**[03:47:59.500] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003267)  
so that my colleague would be able to go over the document.

**[03:48:04.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003268)  
Madam Speaker, what if I file a House resolution in aid of legislation and I will summon the DICT

**[03:48:12.420] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003269)  
and who will be the representatives from META?

**[03:48:15.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003270)  
Sino bang papadala lang?

**[03:48:17.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003271)  
Kali mag-file ako ng House resolution with regard to what happened to Zamboanga massacre.

**[03:48:24.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003272)  
And of course, yung mga kanilang violation.

**[03:48:27.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003273)  
Sino ang pupunta rito from META?

**[03:48:30.940] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003274)  
Anyone from META?

**[03:48:32.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003275)  
Will be held responsible?

**[03:48:34.740] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003276)  
From the DICT, they themselves wrote directly to Mark Zuckerberg to ask him to confront these issues which we are facing.

**[03:48:46.300] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003277)  
Although...

**[03:48:47.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003278)  
There was no response.

**[03:48:49.000] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003279)  
Madam Speaker, I don't think the founder, Mark Zuckerberg, will come here.

**[03:48:54.020] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003280)  
Para sa atin, ganito lang tayo sa kanya.

**[03:48:56.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003281)  
They make a lot of money through Facebook.

**[03:48:59.320] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003282)  
Alam naman nila na puri karamihan is...

**[03:49:03.140] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003283)  
I'm not saying the whole thing, no?

**[03:49:04.840] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003284)  
Because I don't have...

**[03:49:06.300] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003285)  
Myself, I don't have Facebook account, no?

**[03:49:08.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003286)  
Kasi alam ko maraming kalokohan dyan, pero maraming gumagamit at karamihan sa ating mga kababayan

**[03:49:14.980] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003287)  
ay kumikita naman dyan, in fairness.

**[03:49:17.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003288)  
To Facebook, no?

**[03:49:18.900] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003289)  
To META.

**[03:49:19.820] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003290)  
But again, we're talking about responsibility.

**[03:49:23.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003291)  
We're talking about yung kailangan nila maging, ano sila rito, accountable.

**[03:49:29.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003292)  
Yan po ang kailangan natin, Madam Speaker.

**[03:49:33.180] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003293)  
Madam Speaker, though Mark Zuckerberg himself never showed up in countries like Brazil and others,

**[03:49:39.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003294)  
they did send legal representation to confront the issues in other countries.

**[03:49:44.620] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003295)  
So, I assume...

**[03:49:46.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003296)  
Kung magkaroon po tayo ng isang...

**[03:49:47.540] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003297)  
Kung magkaroon po tayo ng isang House Resolution investigating this,

**[03:49:50.460] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003298)  
kailangan na kailangan magpadala ng representation ang META to confront these valid issues from the House of Representatives.

**[03:49:57.780] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003299)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[03:50:02.620] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003300)  
the issue then is not the absence of initiatives,

**[03:50:06.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003301)  
but whether the ICT has sufficient mechanisms to monitor implementation and measure actual results.

**[03:50:14.120] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003302)  
I appreciate the responses of the Department,

**[03:50:17.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003303)  
and the good sponsor.

**[03:50:20.080] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003304)  
However, I remain concerned that there is still insufficient information before this body

**[03:50:27.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003305)  
regarding the actual outcomes of the ICT's anti-scam initiatives.

**[03:50:35.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003306)  
The Department has entered into agreement, established reporting mechanisms,

**[03:50:40.800] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003307)  
and coordinated with private platforms and law enforcement agency.

**[03:50:46.260] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003308)  
But,

**[03:50:47.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003309)  
for purposes of budget review,

**[03:50:50.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003310)  
this Committee must also be able to determine what happened after those actions were initiated.

**[03:50:59.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003311)  
Without that information,

**[03:51:00.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003312)  
it is difficult for Congress to properly assess whether the resources being requested are producing the intended outcomes.

**[03:51:10.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003313)  
Accordingly, Madam Chair,

**[03:51:11.960] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003314)  
until those matters are a satisfactory address,

**[03:51:16.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003315)  
and the Department can...

**[03:51:17.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003316)  
and the Department can present measurables, deliverables.

**[03:51:22.540] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003317)  
Madam Speaker, it pains me, but I have no choice.

**[03:51:29.520] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-003318)  
I move that for consideration of the budget of the Department of Information and Communication Technology be deferred.

**[03:51:40.800] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-003319)  
Madam Speaker,

**[03:51:42.280] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003320)  
Majority Leader,

**[03:51:43.080] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-003321)  
on the part of the majority,

**[03:51:47.540] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-003322)  
we join the minority in the motion to defer the period of interpellations on the proposed budget

**[03:51:55.680] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-003323)  
of the Department of Information and Communications Technology,

**[03:52:00.520] Rep. Jonathan Keith T. Flores** (asr-003324)  
including its attached agencies.

**[03:52:06.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003325)  
Because of the wisdom of the minority,

**[03:52:10.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003326)  
that the plan of the ICT insofar as curbing scams,

**[03:52:17.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003327)  
and victimizing our children on the negative aspects,

**[03:52:22.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003328)  
especially on their behavior,

**[03:52:24.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003329)  
has not been addressed well,

**[03:52:26.920] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003330)  
there is a motion from the minority,

**[03:52:30.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003331)  
duly supported by the majority,

**[03:52:32.980] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003332)  
to defer consideration of the proposed budget of the Department of Information and Communications Technology,

**[03:52:41.040] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003333)  
including its attached agencies.

**[03:52:43.840] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003334)  
Do we hear an objection?

**[03:52:46.460] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003335)  
Hearing none, said motion.

**[03:52:47.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003336)  
Motion is approved.

**[03:52:48.800] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003337)  
Consideration of the budget of DICT,

**[03:52:51.640] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003338)  
the Department of Information and Communications Technology,

**[03:52:55.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003339)  
including its attached agencies,

**[03:52:56.720] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003340)  
hereby deferred.

**[03:52:59.360] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003341)  
Session suspended.

**[04:00:00.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003377)  
May we request the DOLE family to be seated so we can start?

**[04:00:13.040] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003378)  
Session resumed.

**[04:00:15.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003379)  
Majority Leader.

**[04:00:19.000] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-003380)  
Madam Speaker, I move to consider the proposed budget of the Department of Labor and Employment, including its attached agencies.

**[04:00:27.280] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003381)  
An objection? Hearing none.

**[04:00:30.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003382)  
The motion to consider the proposed budget of the Department of Labor and Employment, including its attached agencies, is hereby open.

**[04:00:39.880] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003383)  
Majority Leader.

**[04:00:41.380] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-003384)  
Madam Speaker, may we acknowledge the heads and officials of the Department of Labor and Employment, namely DOLE Secretary Francis Tolentino,

**[04:00:50.320] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-003385)  
TESDA Secretary Jose Francisco Kiko Benitez, Undersecretary Warren Miclat, Undersecretary Felipe Egargo Jr.,

**[04:00:59.500] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-003386)  
Undersecretary Carmela Torres, and we would also like to acknowledge the presence of the heads of attached agencies, regional directors, other officials and employees of DOLE.

**[04:01:12.180] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003387)  
The Department of Labor and Employment family, together with its attached agencies, headed by our very good friend, colleague, and former boss and mentor, Secretary Francis Tolentino,

**[04:01:26.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003389)  
Ang gabay at tatay ng ating mga manggagawa.

**[04:01:29.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003390)  
At kamanggagawa, maraming salamat po sa inyong pagdalo dito sa Kongreso.

**[04:01:34.760] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003391)  
Majority Leader.

**[04:01:37.120] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-003392)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Vice Chair Rommel Angara of the Lone District of Aurora

**[04:01:45.420] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-003393)  
and the Honorable Vice Chair Maria Angela Garcia of the Third District of Bataan to sponsor the proposed budget of DOLE.

**[04:01:54.600] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003394)  
The Honorable Vice Chair Rommel Angara is hereby recognized

**[04:01:59.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003395)  
Sponsor the budget of the Department of Labor and Employment.

**[04:02:02.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003396)  
And of course, the Honorable Vice Chair Maria Angela Garcia, sponsor of the proposed budget of TESDA, are hereby recognized.

**[04:02:11.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003397)  
Majority Leader.

**[04:02:14.920] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003398)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker.

**[04:02:18.420] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003399)  
Ngayong gabing ito, at tayo na po ang huling magkakaroon ng depensa sa ating budget ng ating Department of Labor and Employment,

**[04:02:25.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003400)  
kasama na po ang ating attached agencies, including TESDA.

**[04:02:29.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003401)  
For the NEP of 2027, Department of Labor and Employment has Php 47,057,355,000.

**[04:02:38.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003402)  
Sa aking mga kasamahan dito sa Kongreso, ang ating budget po noong ating 2026 GAA ay Php 61.2 billion.

**[04:02:48.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003403)  
So mayroon po tayong Php 14.1 billion na nabawas sa ating pondo na alam kong napakahalaga pagdating sa pagdating sa pagservisyo ng mandato ng ating Departamento.

**[04:02:59.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003404)  
At that same time, pagbibigay ng mga karagdagang skills to upskill our workforce in the future.

**[04:03:07.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003405)  
Ako ay naniniwala na sa ating mga kasamahan at pagdating ng ating mga susunod pa mga deliberasyon na sana ang hiling po natin ay madagdagan o may balikman lamang yung pondong kinakailangan para lubusang magampanan ng ating Departamento ang kanilang mandato.

**[04:03:23.340] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003406)  
Pangalawa, sa ating bagong Secretary Francis Tolentino, alam po ninyo,

**[04:03:29.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003407)  
ang mga initiatives under his new leadership na kung saan sa aking palagay ay malaki ang maitutulong para sa ating Departamento at sa ating mga laborers and workers, hindi lamang sa NCR, bagkos sa buong Pilipinas.

**[04:03:43.720] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003408)  
Una, ay hinihingi rin niya po sa Kongreso ang karagdagan na sana ay magkaroon ng karagdagang Php 75 million upang sa ating Labor Attorney's Office.

**[04:03:52.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003409)  
Ano po ito, aking mga kasamahan?

**[04:03:54.820] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003410)  
Siyempre po, alam naman po natin na ang PAO ay libre na po sa ating mga manggagawa.

**[04:03:59.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003411)  
Subalit, sa dami po ng kaso na mga kinakaharap ng ating PAO, minabuti ng ating butihing Secretary na mayroong tututok sa ating mga manggagawa, oras na ito ay magkaroon ng free legal assistance, yan po ang ating Labor Attorney's Office in conjunction o kasama po dito yung bagong itatayo na NALJ o National Academy for Labor Justice.

**[04:04:23.960] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003412)  
Kaya sa aking mga kasamahan, I'm sure ito po ay napakahalaga.

**[04:04:29.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003413)  
para ma-implement ngayong 2026 and moving forward hanggang 2027 para siguraduhin natin na ang ating mga workers, hindi lamang sa Metro Manila bagkos sa buong Pilipinas, ay magkaroon ng kahit papaano ay social protection at meron silang masasandalan pagdating ng oras ng kinakailangan. So Madam Speaker, this representation from the Lone District of Aurora is now ready for interpellation together with Maria Angela Garcia for TESDA. Madam Speaker, we're now open for interpellation.

**[04:04:59.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003414)  
Majority Leader.

**[04:05:02.420] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-003415)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Elijah San Fernando of Partylist Kamanggagawa to interpolate on the proposed budget of the DOLE.

**[04:05:11.380] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003416)  
The Honorable Elijah San Fernando is hereby recognized for his interpolation.

**[04:05:17.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003417)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker, at sa ating kaibigan, ang kagalang-galang na Romel Angara, sponsor po ng budget ng Department of Labor and Employment.

**[04:05:27.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003418)  
Malinaw po.

**[04:05:29.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003419)  
Ang mandato ng Department of Labor and Employment.

**[04:05:33.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003420)  
Ito ay ipagtanggol at itaguyod ang karapatan ng mga manggagawa.

**[04:05:41.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003421)  
Diyan pa lamang po, Mr. Sponsor, Madam Speaker, napakabigat na na responsibilidad ang nakaatang sa Department of Labor and Employment.

**[04:05:54.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003422)  
Pero Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[04:05:57.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003423)  
ang malaking tanong ng mga manggagawa,

**[04:06:03.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003424)  
bakit sa hinihinging budget ng Department of Labor and Employment,

**[04:06:09.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003425)  
67% o tumataginting na 14 bilyong piso,

**[04:06:17.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003426)  
ang ilalaan para sa DOLE, Office of the Secretary, particular sa programang tupad, Mr. Sponsor?

**[04:06:27.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003427)  
What is the question, Distinguished Colleague?

**[04:06:33.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003428)  
Bakit po, 67% o tumataginting na 14 bilyong piso, ang ilalaan ng DOLE, Office of the Secretary, sa programang tupad, Mr. Sponsor?

**[04:06:53.120] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003429)  
Thank you for that, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:06:56.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003430)  
Alam mo po,

**[04:06:57.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003431)  
pag dinisek mo po yung ating pondo ng Office of the Secretary,

**[04:07:01.140] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003432)  
kasi pagdating po, doon lang po ilalagak yan.

**[04:07:03.920] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003433)  
Pero eventually, pagdating ng implementation, it will be trickled down to the regional offices,

**[04:07:09.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003434)  
down to provincial levels, per district, per town,

**[04:07:11.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003435)  
para magkaroon po ng employment at tulong sa ating mga kababayan.

**[04:07:16.820] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003436)  
Simple as that, Madam Speaker and ating Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:07:20.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003437)  
So, Mr. Sponsor, Madam Speaker,

**[04:07:23.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003438)  
binibigyang matwid ngayon ng Department of Labor and Employment,

**[04:07:27.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003439)  
na mahigit kalahati ng budget na hinihingi ng DOLE for the Office of the Secretary,

**[04:07:34.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003440)  
ay ilalaan sa tupad kung ang isa sa malaking mandato ng DOLE

**[04:07:40.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003441)  
ay proteksyonan, ipagtanggol, at itaguyod ang karapatan ng mga manggagawa.

**[04:07:45.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003442)  
Papaano maiintindihan ng mga manggagawa na ginagampanan ng DOLE

**[04:07:51.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003443)  
ang napakaraming mga responsibilidad na ito

**[04:07:54.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003444)  
kung 67% ng budget,

**[04:07:57.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003445)  
ay ilalagak natin sa Office of the Secretary,

**[04:08:01.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003446)  
partikular sa programang tupad.

**[04:08:04.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003447)  
Ang binabanggit po ng butihing sponsor, Madam Speaker,

**[04:08:07.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003448)  
ay para daw po makapag-provide ng mga emergency na trabaho.

**[04:08:12.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003449)  
Tama po ba ito, Mr. Sponsor?

**[04:08:13.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003450)  
That is correct, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:08:17.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003451)  
So, malinaw po ba na refleksyon ito ng labor policy ng ating pamahalaan

**[04:08:23.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003452)  
kung saan mas binibigyang prioridad ang mga emergency jobs?

**[04:08:27.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003453)  
Sa halip ng lumikha ng mga trabahong pangmatagalan at dekalidad,

**[04:08:32.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003454)  
Mr. Sponsor?

**[04:08:34.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003455)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:08:36.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003456)  
Yes, alam mo, yung tupad was started during the pandemic.

**[04:08:40.460] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003457)  
And I agree with you.

**[04:08:41.800] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003458)  
Pero you also have to understand, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:08:45.460] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003459)  
sa ating bansa ng Pilipinas, unfortunately,

**[04:08:48.480] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003460)  
nagkaroon tayo ng mga natural disasters.

**[04:08:51.040] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003461)  
Alam mo, pag nangyari ito, even the local governments,

**[04:08:54.080] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003462)  
I came from the local government,

**[04:08:55.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003463)  
minsan karamihan dyan,

**[04:08:57.100] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003464)  
walang pera,

**[04:08:57.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003465)  
para pang salba sa ating mga naaksidente, binagyo, so on and so forth.

**[04:09:03.940] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003466)  
Even pag nagkaroon ng programa para sa ating transport group,

**[04:09:07.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003467)  
papasok po ang tupad.

**[04:09:08.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003468)  
Yes, I get your point na sinasabi po ninyo na dapat lamang ba ay trabaho natin.

**[04:09:14.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003469)  
You also have to understand o dapat po nating alalanin,

**[04:09:17.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003470)  
puwera sa polisiya na paggawa ng formats, programs ng DOLE,

**[04:09:21.620] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003471)  
ay kinakailangan na sa mandato rin ito ay magkaroon ng trabaho para sa ating mamamayang Pilipino.

**[04:09:27.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003472)  
It is not per se na kinakailangan natin na ito ay patuloy na ginagawa,

**[04:09:34.380] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003473)  
pero unfortunately, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:09:37.380] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003474)  
ito po ay at the end of the day,

**[04:09:39.940] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003475)  
kada probinsya at ang buong bansa ay talaga minsan,

**[04:09:42.660] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003476)  
unfortunately, ay nangangailangan ng ayuda at tulong para sa ating mga mamamayan.

**[04:09:47.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003477)  
Maganda po na nabanggit, Madam Speaker,

**[04:09:50.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003478)  
ng ating butihing sponsor,

**[04:09:52.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003479)  
na meron tayong mga isinasaalang-alang na mga konsiderasyon gaya ng krisis.

**[04:09:57.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003480)  
Ngunit, Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[04:10:01.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003481)  
hindi po tinatanggap ng representasyong ito na gagamitin dahilan ang krisis

**[04:10:06.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003482)  
para sa paglobo ng budget ng tupad.

**[04:10:10.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003483)  
In fact, Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[04:10:13.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003484)  
makikita natin,

**[04:10:15.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003485)  
sa loob ng sampung taon mula 2016,

**[04:10:21.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003486)  
hindi nagbago, virtually the same,

**[04:10:24.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003487)  
ang pondong inilalaan

**[04:10:27.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003488)  
ng mga trabaho o mandato

**[04:10:30.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003489)  
ng Dole Office of the Secretary

**[04:10:33.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003490)  
samantalang kitang-kita

**[04:10:34.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003491)  
ang papalubo at papalaking bilang

**[04:10:37.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003492)  
ng inilalagak natin sa tupad.

**[04:10:40.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003493)  
So, hindi po maaaring sabihin

**[04:10:42.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003494)  
na dahil sa krisis, Madam Speaker,

**[04:10:45.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003495)  
dahil bago pa man magkaroon ng krisis,

**[04:10:48.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003496)  
ang Dole, batay sa kanyang budget,

**[04:10:51.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003497)  
papalaki ng papalaki ang inilalagak sa tupad

**[04:10:54.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003498)  
kaysa palakihin ang budget

**[04:10:56.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003499)  
doon sa iba pa.

**[04:10:57.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003500)  
Ano ang mga funksyon ng Department of Labor and Employment?

**[04:11:00.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003501)  
Mr. Sponsor.

**[04:11:01.720] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003502)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:11:04.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003503)  
I'd like to correct the facts.

**[04:11:05.480] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003504)  
Sa Office of the Secretary, it's not 67%, but 52%.

**[04:11:09.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003505)  
For the record, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:11:12.660] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003506)  
and secondly,

**[04:11:13.920] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003507)  
tama po, but you also have to understand,

**[04:11:16.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003508)  
tupad per se is not just tupad per se.

**[04:11:18.280] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003509)  
It also includes the government internship program

**[04:11:21.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003510)  
na kung saan yung ating mga fresh graduates

**[04:11:23.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003511)  
ay papasok sa ating mga gobyerno

**[04:11:25.540] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003512)  
upang sagayoy mag-gain sila ng employability

**[04:11:27.820] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003513)  
at mag-gain sila ng employability

**[04:11:27.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003514)  
alam naman po natin dito sa ating bansa

**[04:11:29.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003515)  
na kung saan karamihan ng mga ating employers

**[04:11:33.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003516)  
ay nangangailangan ng kinakailangan experience

**[04:11:35.880] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003517)  
para ito ay sila'y ma-hire.

**[04:11:37.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003518)  
And in fact, sabi ko nga,

**[04:11:39.200] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003519)  
because of this situation,

**[04:11:41.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003520)  
ay ito po ay maraming mga fresh graduates

**[04:11:43.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003521)  
ang makikinabang dito.

**[04:11:45.040] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003522)  
Sabi ko nga, ang isa sa mandato

**[04:11:46.360] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003523)  
ng ating departemento

**[04:11:47.820] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003524)  
ay mag-create ng ating mga trabaho

**[04:11:49.540] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003525)  
not only for the normal na manggagawa

**[04:11:53.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003526)  
but also sa ating mga fresh graduates

**[04:11:56.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003527)  
dito sa ating bansa.

**[04:11:56.840] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003528)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[04:11:57.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003529)  
Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[04:12:00.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003530)  
binabanggit po ng DOLE na hindi 67%,

**[04:12:03.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003531)  
kundi 52% lamang.

**[04:12:05.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003532)  
Pero malinaw po, doon sa budget ng DOLE,

**[04:12:07.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003533)  
wala naman kasing distinksyon

**[04:12:09.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003534)  
ang TUPAD at GIP.

**[04:12:11.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003535)  
So kapag sinuma natin,

**[04:12:13.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003536)  
67% ang nasa TUPAD, hindi 52%.

**[04:12:17.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003537)  
Be that as it may, Madam Sponsor, Mr. Speaker,

**[04:12:20.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003538)  
ang punto natin, mahigit kalahati pa rin

**[04:12:23.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003539)  
ng pondo ang inilalagak sa TUPAD.

**[04:12:25.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003540)  
Kaya ako po ito tinanong,

**[04:12:26.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003541)  
Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[04:12:28.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003542)  
kasi sa 2025 COA findings,

**[04:12:32.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003543)  
sangkatutak na irregularidad ang nakita sa TUPAD.

**[04:12:37.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003544)  
Halimbawa, Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[04:12:40.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003545)  
base sa audit findings ng COA,

**[04:12:42.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003546)  
may mga benepisaryo ang TUPAD

**[04:12:45.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003547)  
na nakakatanggap ng payout,

**[04:12:48.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003548)  
hindi lang po isang beses,

**[04:12:50.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003549)  
hindi lang dalawang beses,

**[04:12:52.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003550)  
kundi tatlo hanggang apat na beses,

**[04:12:56.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003551)  
na nakakatanggap ng payout.

**[04:12:59.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003552)  
Mismong DOLE ay nagva-violate ng guidelines o rule

**[04:13:05.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003553)  
pagdating sa TUPAD.

**[04:13:07.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003554)  
One per family per year.

**[04:13:10.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003555)  
Tapos ngayon, nagre-request uli ang Department of Labor and Employment,

**[04:13:14.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003556)  
Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[04:13:16.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003557)  
ng pagkalaki-laking budget na naman sa TUPAD

**[04:13:20.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003558)  
na hindi pa nare-resolve ba ang sistema dito

**[04:13:25.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003559)  
sa mga benepisaryo ng TUPAD.

**[04:13:28.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003560)  
Mr. Sponsor.

**[04:13:30.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003561)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:13:33.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003562)  
Yes, to answer directly sa ating katanungan ng ating sponsor,

**[04:13:38.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003563)  
I clarify that also to the Department.

**[04:13:41.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003564)  
Siguro ang tinutukoy niyo po dito ay yung 1,275

**[04:13:44.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003565)  
na naka-finlag ng COA, na Commission on Audit,

**[04:13:47.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003566)  
na kung saan nakatanggap ito di umano ng dalawang beses.

**[04:13:51.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003567)  
For the record, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:13:54.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003568)  
pinanong na po natin kung sa regional director,

**[04:13:56.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003569)  
kung saan region ito ay nangyari base doon sa ating COA report.

**[04:14:00.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003570)  
At doon po sa sinasabing 1,275, may explanation po dyan at ito po iyon.

**[04:14:06.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003571)  
Una, yung 1,275 na binabanggit ay nagkamali po yun sa database lamang.

**[04:14:12.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003572)  
Because kasama po doon sa mga 1,275 na iyon,

**[04:14:17.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003573)  
let me reiterate, Mr. Distinguished Colleague, na yes, tama ka doon sa sinabi mong isang beses lamang.

**[04:14:23.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003574)  
Pwede magtupad. Pero there's an exception to it.

**[04:14:27.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003575)  
Pag may kalamidad, pwedeng umulit po. That's for the record.

**[04:14:30.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003576)  
Pero nung ito ay nating nadiskubre doon sa database po, nagkamali lamang sa database.

**[04:14:37.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003577)  
At ito ay naayos na po natin sa Commission on Audit.

**[04:14:40.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003578)  
At kinalabasan po dito na 15 lamang out of the 1,275 ang nagkaroon ng doubling payment.

**[04:14:49.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003579)  
And for the record, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:14:52.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003580)  
doon sa labing limang iyon, sinigurado rin po natin, siyempre, sabi ko nga po, ang dole ay laging susunod.

**[04:15:00.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003581)  
sa alituntunin ng batas at lalo na sa sarili niyang requirement na dapat isang beses lamang bayaran ang isang nagmembro o nagtrabaho para sa tupad.

**[04:15:11.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003582)  
And for information of the body, kahit i-double check po natin, yung labing limang recipient po yun ay nakuha na po muli yung ating pondo

**[04:15:21.100] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-003583)  
dahil nagkamali nga po at yan po ay ni-report na po namin sa COA, Madam Speaker at Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:15:27.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-003584)  
Mr. Sponsor, Madam Speaker, hindi lang po 1,275 beneficiaries. Batay po sa COA findings, meron din pong nakakuha sa Saksargen,

Chapter 3,

Section 4 of the IRR,

any wage order

ng Department of Labor and Employment

ang nag-announce

ng Department of Labor and Employment

ang Wage Order No. 27

ng Wage Order No.

28?

**[04:37:28.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004093)  
Madam Speaker,

**[04:37:29.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004094)  
Distinguse Colleague,

**[04:37:30.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004095)

**[04:37:33.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004096)

**[04:37:34.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004097)  
yung Wage Order No. 27, alam naman po natin at alam din ng ating distinguished colleague na

**[04:37:40.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004098)  
nagkaroon una ng TRO, pangalawa, nagkaroon ng injunction.

**[04:37:43.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004099)  
Ang tinatanong niyo po

**[04:37:45.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004100)
