**[04:37:47.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004101)  
The answer is no.

**[04:37:49.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004102)  
Alam mo po, ginagalang

**[04:37:51.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004103)  
ng Department

**[04:37:52.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004104)  
of Labor and Employment

**[04:37:53.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004105)  
ang ating legal proceedings,

**[04:37:54.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004106)  
which we abide by it.

**[04:37:55.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004107)  
But our official stand, para alam po ng ating mga kababayan at ng ating 1.1 million na manggagawa sa Metro Manila,

**[04:38:03.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004108)  
hindi po binibitawan ng Department of Labor and Employment ang Wage Order 27.

**[04:38:10.340] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004109)  
In fact, ang ating sekretary pa mismo ang nag-file sa ating mga korte na kung saan ang tindig o opisyal na tindig ng ating Departamento

**[04:38:21.720] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004110)  
together with the Office of the Solicitor General na dapat ito ay legal kasi walang jurisdiction ang korte

**[04:38:30.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004111)  
at dapat ito ay ipatupad ng ating mga korts at dapat sana ayaw makialam.

**[04:38:36.620] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004112)  
So in fact, yung ating Wage Order 28 na 60 pesos, and ito po, importanteng-importante po ito, Madam Speaker. Bakit?

**[04:38:45.480] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004113)  
Yung Wage Order 28, 60 pesos.

**[04:38:49.320] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004114)  
Doon sa Wage Order 27,

**[04:38:51.720] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004115)  
85 pesos.

**[04:38:53.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004116)  
Pero, nakakalimutan banggitin doon lagi na sa Wage Order 27, sa taong 2026, ay it's also 60 pesos.

**[04:39:02.520] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004117)  
Patunay lamang, patapusin mo ako, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:39:05.640] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004118)  
sapagkat sa pagkakataong yun, makikita natin na ang Departamento ay nagtatrabaho. Bakit?

**[04:39:14.640] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004119)  
Simpleng-simple lang naman, kung kami walang malasakit o hindi ginagampanan ang aming tungkulin,

**[04:39:20.760] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004120)  
[unclear] sana,

**[04:39:21.720] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004121)  
pinabayaan na lang namin yung Wage Order 27.

**[04:39:24.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004122)  
Subalit hindi.

**[04:39:25.620] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004123)  
Sa mandato na binigay ng Kongreso at Senado at ang taong bayan,

**[04:39:29.360] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004124)  
ang ating Wage Board, kumilos, nagpulong, nagpublic hearing,

**[04:39:34.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004125)  
para sa gayo yung magkaroon ng economic relief o katumbas ng ating 60 pesos na dapat dagdag sa Wage Order number 27.

**[04:39:42.960] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004126)  
So yung Wage Order number 28 is separate from Wage Order 27.

**[04:39:49.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004127)  
Yun lang po, Madam Speaker, at least.

**[04:39:51.080] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004128)  
Thank you.

**[04:39:51.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004129)  
Madam Speaker, this is of paramount importance.

**[04:39:56.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004130)  
At ito po ang pagkakataon, Madam Speaker, na maliwanagan ang milyong-milyong mga manggagawa.

**[04:40:02.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004131)  
So with the indulgence of our distinguished sponsor and Madam Speaker,

**[04:40:08.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004132)  
just a few more questions, Madam Speaker.

**[04:40:10.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004133)  
Just one more question.

**[04:40:12.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004134)  
Clarification on that matter.

**[04:40:14.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004135)  
Binabanggit po ng Department of Labor and Employment na hindi isinusuko ang Wage Order 27,

**[04:40:20.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004136)  
but in effect,

**[04:40:21.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004137)  
sinusuko nila.

**[04:40:22.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004138)  
Malinaw ang sinasabi.

**[04:40:24.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004139)  
If there is no bond, there is no injunction.

**[04:40:27.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004140)  
That is the rules of court.

**[04:40:29.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004141)  
Walang legal impediment, sabi ng Office of the Secretary General,

**[04:40:33.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004142)  
para ipatupad ang Wage Order 27.

**[04:40:36.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004143)  
Binabanggit ng distinguished sponsor, ipatutupad ang Wage Order 28 bilang pamamaraan para ibigay sa mga manggagawa

**[04:40:44.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004144)  
yung 60 pesos mula doon sa first tranche.

**[04:40:49.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004145)  
Pero hindi po yun ang punto.

**[04:40:51.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004146)  
Mula July 25, dahil walang hurisdiksyon ang Korte, kayo na po mismo ang nagsabi.

**[04:40:57.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004147)  
Dapat matanggap na ng bawat isang minimum wage earner yung 60 pesos.

**[04:41:02.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004148)  
Ibig sabihin, may kakulangan, may utang sa mga manggagawa na mahigit 3,000 piso.

**[04:41:07.980] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004149)  
Ito po ang binabanggit natin, Mr. Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[04:41:11.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004150)  
Hindi po maaaring isuplant ng Wage Order 28 yung Wage Order 27.

**[04:41:16.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004151)  
So ang tanong dito, nilabag na nga ng DOLE.

**[04:41:20.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004152)  
Ang sarili niyang guidelines na isang order lang ang ilalabas per year.

**[04:41:25.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004153)  
Tapos sinasabi niya na gumawa siya ng pamamaraan para maibigay yung unang tranche, 60 pesos.

**[04:41:32.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004154)  
Pero kung susumahin natin, 60 pesos lang ang dagdag-saud sa Wage Order 28.

**[04:41:38.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004155)  
Ibig sabihin po ba, maglalabas ang DOLE ng panibagong Wage Order

**[04:41:42.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004156)  
para i-cover yung natitira mula doon sa Wage Order 27?

**[04:41:47.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004157)  
Mas pinapagulo ng DOLE.

**[04:41:50.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004158)  
ang kanyang buhay at ang kanyang sarili, pati ang mga manggagawa.

**[04:41:55.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004159)  
Naiintindihan ko po ang binabanggit ng ating butihing sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[04:42:00.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004160)  
Sasabihin nila na pamamaraan.

**[04:42:02.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004161)  
Pero hindi po makuha ng mga manggagawa ang tindig ng Department of Labor and Employment

**[04:42:07.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004162)  
na hindi nito bibitawan at isusuko ang Wage Order 27

**[04:42:11.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004163)  
at ipapatupad nito ang Wage Order 27 na malinaw na nakasulat sa batas.

**[04:42:16.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004164)  
DOLE na lang po ang hindi naniniwala na kaya niyang ipatupad.

**[04:42:20.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004165)  
Ang Wage Order, Madam Speaker.

**[04:42:23.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004166)  
Madam Speaker, I will end here.

**[04:42:26.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004167)  
Ang aming paniniwala po, kung hindi kayang tindigan ng Department of Labor and Employment

**[04:42:32.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004168)  
ang karapatan ng mga manggagawa,

**[04:42:34.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004169)  
it is in our firm position that the Department of Labor and Employment

**[04:42:39.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004170)  
should not ask this August Chamber for any budget

**[04:42:43.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004171)  
dahil pinakikita at pinatutunayan lang nito sa mga manggagawa

**[04:42:47.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004172)  
na ang DOLE ay manhid, inutil at bingi.

**[04:42:50.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004173)  
Sa pakiramdam ng mga manggagawa.

**[04:42:53.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004174)  
Intindihin nyo kung saan nang gagaling ang mga manggagawa.

**[04:42:56.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004175)  
Para sa inyo, maliit na halaga lang ang 60 pesos.

**[04:42:59.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004176)  
Pero sa bawat isang minimum wage earner na naririnig,

**[04:43:03.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004177)  
ang ating diskusyon ngayon, malaking bagay ang 3,000 pisong inagaw

**[04:43:08.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004178)  
at pinagkait sa mga minimum wage earner.

**[04:43:11.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004179)  
Yun po ang pinatutungkulan ng representasyong ito.

**[04:43:15.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004180)  
And at the appropriate time,

**[04:43:16.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004181)  
if the Department of Labor and Employment,

**[04:43:20.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004182)  
will not change its position

**[04:43:22.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004183)  
at mananatili siya na matigas sa kanyang paninindigan

**[04:43:28.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004184)  
na maglalabas ng bagong wage order sa halip na tindigan

**[04:43:32.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004185)  
ang lumang wage order,

**[04:43:34.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004186)  
this representation will make that motion

**[04:43:37.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004187)  
at the appropriate time

**[04:43:38.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004188)  
na ilagak na lang natin ang pondo ng Department of Labor and Employment

**[04:43:43.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004189)  
excluding TESDA.

**[04:43:47.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004190)  
Ilagak na lang yan sa ibang mga functions

**[04:43:50.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004191)  
ng ating gobyerno.

**[04:43:52.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004192)  
Hindi po napatunayan ng Department of Labor and Employment

**[04:43:55.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004193)  
na kaya niyang tindigan ang karapatan ng mga manggagawa.

**[04:43:58.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004194)  
Mr. Sponsor, maraming salamat po sa inyong pagbigyan.

**[04:44:02.200] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004195)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:44:03.940] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004196)  
pwede mo ba akong pagbigyan na sagutin yung huling statement mo?

**[04:44:06.540] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004197)  
For the record, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[04:44:09.420] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004198)  
alam po ninyo, kanina binanggit ko po

**[04:44:12.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004199)  
na ang posisyon ng Departamento o DOLE

**[04:44:16.320] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004200)  
ay we impose na tinitindigan po namin

**[04:44:20.540] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004201)  
ang wage order 27.

**[04:44:22.840] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004202)  
Klaro po yun. It's on record na binanggit na po natin.

**[04:44:25.760] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004203)  
And kahit tanongin mo po lahat dito,

**[04:44:27.640] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004204)  
yung po ay on record.

**[04:44:29.760] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004205)  
Kaya't hindi po namin pipwedeng tanggapin

**[04:44:31.380] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004206)  
with all due respect to my friend, Congressman Eli,

**[04:44:36.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004207)  
sapagkat sabi ko nga rin kanina,

**[04:44:38.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004208)  
kung kami talaga ay walang malasakit sa ating mga manggagawa,

**[04:44:43.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004209)  
alisana kami po ay hindi na po kumilos.

**[04:44:45.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004210)  
Subalit,

**[04:44:46.120] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004211)  
sabi ko nga kanina sa pangungunan ng ating bagong sekretary,

**[04:44:49.740] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004212)  
we respect the legal proceedings.

**[04:44:52.800] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004213)  
Yan, alam mo yan.

**[04:44:54.800] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004214)  
Alam mo yan.

**[04:44:56.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004215)  
Nakumbakit.

**[04:44:57.880] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004216)  
Pero pinaninindigan po namin

**[04:45:00.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004217)  
na ang Wage Order 27, we cannot prejudge kung ano sasabihin ng ating hudikotura.

**[04:45:06.520] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004218)  
Nire-respeto namin yan.

**[04:45:07.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004219)  
Pero ang sabi namin nga noon ng Dolly at Office ng OSG,

**[04:45:11.120] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004220)  
ay dapat ito ay implemented ng July 2025 o 2026,

**[04:45:15.840] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004221)  
na dapat hindi nakikialam ang Korte.

**[04:45:18.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004222)  
At yan naman po, alam mo rin po yan,

**[04:45:20.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004223)  
na klaro sa Article 126 ng Labor Code na yun ang ating sinasabi.

**[04:45:25.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004224)  
Kaya sabi ko nga, Madam Speaker, for clarification,

**[04:45:27.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004225)  
sa lahat na nakikinig ng ating mga gawa na 1.1 million sa NCR.

**[04:45:32.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004226)  
Una, yung 60 pesos po ng Wage 27 at 28 ay identical.

**[04:45:40.320] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004227)  
Pag sinabi mong nabawasan, hindi po.

**[04:45:42.920] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004228)  
I would like to quote you, my friend, Ellie, simple math.

**[04:45:47.140] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004229)  
60 minus 60 is the same value.

**[04:45:52.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004230)  
Ngayon kung tatanungin mo, kanina,

**[04:45:54.560] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004231)  
na kung magre-release na naman ng Wage,

**[04:45:57.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004232)  
ang Wage Order para ito ay sa susunod na mga hakbang.

**[04:46:00.800] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004233)  
And I know na alam mo po yan,

**[04:46:02.740] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004234)  
para klaro po sa ating nakikinig,

**[04:46:04.820] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004235)  
kasi marami po yan at malaki po yan.

**[04:46:07.340] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004236)  
Yung 60 pesos na po yun, pag minultiply mo sa 26 days,

**[04:46:10.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004237)  
that's 1,560.

**[04:46:13.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004238)  
Yes, inaamin ng Dolly at ng Wage Board

**[04:46:15.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004239)  
na hindi lahat ng problema ay masusulusyonan doon sa 1,560.

**[04:46:21.720] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004240)  
Pero malaking bagay ito.

**[04:46:23.140] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004241)  
At ito po, uulitin ko po, for the record,

**[04:46:25.420] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004242)  
ay tinitindigan.

**[04:46:27.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004243)  
Nang Departamento kasama ang OSG.

**[04:46:31.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004244)  
Sabi ko nga po, once that document from the Judiciary,

**[04:46:37.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004245)  
binanggit na lang ating mahal na Secretary Francis Torrentino,

**[04:46:41.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004246)  
ipapatupad po natin ang mga Wage Orders

**[04:46:43.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004247)  
na ating pinanilindigan para sa ating mga manggagawa

**[04:46:47.020] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004248)  
sa NCR o sa buong Pilipinas sa mga darating na panahon.

**[04:46:51.080] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004249)  
Sana ay kahit pa paano,

**[04:46:52.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004250)  
nasagot namin yung itanong Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:46:57.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-004251)  
Maraming salamat, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[04:46:59.280] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004252)  
Thank you very much, Congressman Eli San Fernando.

**[04:47:03.460] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004253)  
Majority Leader?

**[04:47:05.380] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-004254)  
Madam Speaker, I move that we recognize

**[04:47:07.260] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-004255)  
the Honorable Antonio Tinio of the ACT Teachers Party-list

**[04:47:10.520] Rep. Marie Bernadette G. Escudero** (asr-004256)  
to interpolate on the proposed budget.

**[04:47:13.300] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004257)  
The Honorable Tinio is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[04:47:17.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004258)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker,

**[04:47:20.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004259)  
sa Distinguished Sponsor at sa Department of Labor and Employment.

**[04:47:24.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004260)  
Ilang mga katanungan po.

**[04:47:26.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004261)  
Well, of course,

**[04:47:27.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004262)  
una, dahil ahensya po ito ng Department of Labor and Employment,

**[04:47:31.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004263)  
of course, ang tanong natin ay kaugnay sa sahod.

**[04:47:37.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004264)  
At dapat meron po tayong graphic dyan.

**[04:47:44.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004265)  
So, ayan, of course.

**[04:47:46.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004266)  
Of course, usapin ng minimum wage.

**[04:47:48.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004267)  
At alam natin, ang umiiral na patakaran at batas at patakaran ngayon,

**[04:47:52.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004268)  
regional, ang setting ng minimum wage.

**[04:47:55.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004269)  
So, dito, sinasummarize, galing sa IBO nito,

**[04:48:00.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004270)  
sinasummarize yung mga regional wage levels per region.

**[04:48:08.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004271)  
Of course, ang pinakamataas dyan ay sa NCR.

**[04:48:18.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004272)  
Actually, hindi nga updated yan.

**[04:48:20.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004273)  
That particular graphic,

**[04:48:23.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004274)  
ang mga rates ngayon,

**[04:48:25.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004275)  
alam naman natin, for NCR,

**[04:48:28.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004276)  
it's already 755.

**[04:48:31.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004277)  
No?

**[04:48:37.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004278)  
Pinakamataas ang sa NCR

**[04:48:39.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004279)  
at dito lumalabas na pinakabababa ang sa BARM.

**[04:48:48.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004280)  
Sa BARM po, 436 pesos ang minimum wage.

**[04:48:54.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004281)  
At,

**[04:48:54.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004282)  
kung,

**[04:48:55.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004283)  
kung i-a-average,

**[04:48:57.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004284)  
so, may range

**[04:48:58.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004285)  
from 755 to 436,

**[04:49:04.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004286)  
average 514.

**[04:49:06.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004287)  
Pero sa totoo lang, yung 514,

**[04:49:08.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004288)  
mathematical, ano lang yan, no?

**[04:49:11.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004289)  
Artifact, dahil wala namang region na ganyan ang minimum wage.

**[04:49:15.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004290)  
Pero just to give you an idea,

**[04:49:16.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004291)  
nung range ng minimum wage sa

**[04:49:20.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004292)  
iba't-ibang lugar sa Pilipinas,

**[04:49:24.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004293)  
dahil nga regionalized.

**[04:49:26.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004294)  
Okay.

**[04:49:27.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004295)  
Pero,

**[04:49:28.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004296)  
sa totoo lang, hindi ganyang kasimple

**[04:49:31.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004297)  
ang totoong lagay

**[04:49:34.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004298)  
ng sahod sa buong bansa.

**[04:49:37.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004299)  
Alam din yan ang mga kasamahan natin sa DOLE dahil

**[04:49:41.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004300)  
kung titignan natin ang bawat regyon,

**[04:49:44.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004301)  
iba-iba ang umiiral na patakaran at

**[04:49:50.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004302)  
ang task ng pagpapasweldo sa mga regyon.

**[04:49:53.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004303)  
So, for example, actually,

**[04:49:58.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004304)  
iba-iba pala.

**[04:50:01.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004305)  
Actually, mas ngayon ko lang talaga na naintindihan.

**[04:50:07.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004306)  
Yung malawak na pagkakaiba ng mga sweldo,

**[04:50:12.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004307)  
hindi lang sa pagitan ng iba't-ibang regyon,

**[04:50:15.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004308)  
kung hindi sa loob mismo ng isang regyon,

**[04:50:17.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004309)  
pwedeng nagkakaiba-iba po ito.

**[04:50:20.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004310)  
For example, ayan, sa ating sponsor.

**[04:50:22.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004311)  
Sa Region 3 pala, hindi pala totoo na meron lang isang

**[04:50:27.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004312)  
regional minimum wage rate dahil regionalized ang wage setting.

**[04:50:32.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004313)  
Hindi pala totoo yun.

**[04:50:34.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004314)  
Dahil, for example, sa Central Luzon,

**[04:50:37.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004315)  
eh ang patakaran dyan ay

**[04:50:44.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004316)  
sa Region 3,

**[04:50:48.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004317)  
meron pong sweldo,

**[04:50:50.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004318)  
para sa buong regyon.

**[04:50:55.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004319)  
Tapos, merong particular na sweldo

**[04:50:59.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004320)  
para sa probinsya lang ng Aurora.

**[04:51:02.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004321)  
Tama po ba yun?

**[04:51:04.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004322)  
So, sa Region 3 po,

**[04:51:08.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004323)  
600 pesos ang rate,

**[04:51:12.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004324)  
pero pagdating sa Aurora,

**[04:51:14.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004325)  
ay lumalabas na,

**[04:51:18.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004326)  
baka ano ba ito?

**[04:51:22.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004327)  
Is it 515?

**[04:51:29.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004328)  
Let me check.

**[04:51:30.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004329)  
Nakakalito na rin po eh.

**[04:51:31.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004330)  
O kasi iba-ibang nga.

**[04:51:34.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004331)  
Can I answer that shortly?

**[05:05:29.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004739)

**[05:05:36.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004740)

**[05:05:39.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004741)

**[05:05:40.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004742)  
At hindi ba panahon na para ma-abolish ang sistema nito ng regional wage setting?

**[05:05:50.840] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004743)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, I get your point.

**[05:05:54.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004744)  
Distinguished colleague, na pag talon mo sa isang lugar na isang bayan ay iba na yung rate.

**[05:06:02.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004745)  
And you gave perfect examples of those in which.

**[05:06:06.480] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004746)  
But the thing is, the mandate which was given by Congress sa Department of Labor and Employment,

**[05:06:13.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004747)  
ay kami naman po ay sumusunod sa alituntunin sa pag-set ng minimum wage.

**[05:06:17.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004748)  
One, yung binigit niyo po kanina ay patungo dun sa we have to also take in consideration

**[05:06:22.940] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004749)  
sa dun sa ating income classes.

**[05:06:24.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004750)  
ng Department of Finance.

**[05:06:26.740] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004751)  
And the most important one is yung productivity.

**[05:06:30.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004752)  
Iba-iba po kasi yung kada bayan, kada reyon, kada probinsya.

**[05:06:35.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004753)  
And patukol naman po dun sa huling statement po ninyo,

**[05:06:40.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004754)  
na kung napapanahon na po ba pag-abolish dun sa mga different rates.

**[05:06:45.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004755)  
Again, the official stand of Department of Labor and Employment patungo dun sa ating minimum wage setting

**[05:06:51.940] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004756)  
or the reach.

**[05:06:54.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004757)  
RA 6727 or Wage Rationalization Act ay we submit to the wisdom ng ating Kongreso at Senado.

**[05:07:05.860] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004758)  
Once halimbawang nakapagpasa po ng isang bill or maging ganap na isang batas,

**[05:07:11.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004759)  
the Department will fully implement kung sakasakaling ito ay maging isang ganap na batas,

**[05:07:17.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-004760)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:07:19.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004761)  
Well, thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[05:07:20.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004762)  
Ang dinidiin po natin talaga, yung patakalan na mismo,

**[05:07:24.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004763)

**[05:08:36.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004775)

the answer is yes, tatlong beses na pong nakasama sa pagpupulong ang ating Department of Labor and Employment and tinitignan ko po yung oras, hindi po bukas, mamaya po meron na pong pagpupulong at dadalo po muli ang ating Departamento ng Labor and Employment.

**[05:30:39.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005317)

**[05:30:54.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005318)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, your question is kung may kasunod pang wage order?

**[05:31:01.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005318-split-2)  
Outside NCR.

**[05:31:03.100] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005318-split-3)  
Outside NCR? Yes.

**[05:31:04.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005318-split-4)  
Akin na yung

**[05:31:09.960] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005319)  
Ito po, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, ang mga rehyong pinakamalapit na anibersaaryo ng kanilang kasulukoy ang wage orders ay Regions 7, 4A, and 3.

**[05:31:21.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005320)  
At region 3. At babasahin ko po yung anibersaary dates para at least klaro po yung ating kung kailan.

**[05:31:29.340] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005321)  
Yung anibersaary date po ng 7 is October 4, 2026. Region 4A is October 5 of 2026. And region 3 is October 30 of 2026.

**[05:31:41.760] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005322)  
Ngayon, ang scheduled date of consultations, it varies.

**[05:31:48.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005323)  
At siguro po yung ating date of publication.

**[05:31:51.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005324)  
At siguro po yung ating date of publication notice of public hearings. Sa ating Region 7 was August 5, 2026.

**[05:31:57.940] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005325)  
Region 4A was August 7 of 2026. And region 3 was September 19 of 2026.

**[05:32:07.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005326)  
And the date of public hearings, again, Region 7 is August 24 and 25 of 2026.

**[05:32:13.640] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005327)  
Also, September 3 and 4 of the year, again, 2026.

**[05:32:17.320] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005328)  
Patungo naman po sa Region 4A, again, August 25, 2026.

**[05:32:21.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005329)  
And 15 and 17 of September, 2026.

**[05:32:27.840] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005330)  
Last region, Region 3 po, October 5, 14, 20, and 21 of 2026.

**[05:32:33.420] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005331)  
November 5, 9, and 11 of 2026.

**[05:32:36.080] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005332)  
So, yung tatlong regiyon po, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, ang mga nakatakdang mga wage orders sa mga susunod.

**[05:32:42.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005333)

**[05:32:46.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005334)

**[05:32:51.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005335)

**[05:32:58.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005336)  
Bukod pa dyan, ay lakasan din ang boses tungkol sa pagtatanggal ng mga patong-patong na buwis na pinapataw ngayon sa ating mga kababayan,

**[05:33:08.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005337)

**[05:33:13.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005338)

**[05:33:18.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005339)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Speaker, sa...

**[05:33:21.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005340)  
Report na nasubmit sa atin ng DOLE, mula 2022 hanggang 2025,

**[05:33:27.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005341)  
nasa 50.05 milyon ang nakolekta na penalties mula sa mga establishments na lumabag sa OSH standards at naideposito na sa Bureau of Treasury.

**[05:33:41.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005342)

**[05:33:47.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005343)

**[05:33:51.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005344)

**[05:33:54.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005345)

**[05:34:04.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005346)

**[05:34:11.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005347)  
ay wala pa pong naka-airmark na pondo na tiyak na magagamit para sa mahal.

**[05:34:21.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005348)

**[05:34:23.340] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005349)

**[05:34:25.520] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005350)

**[05:34:31.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005351)  
And sadly, kailangan po ito ng...

**[05:34:34.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005352)  
Kaya po ito hindi nagagamit ang inyong katanungan.

**[05:34:37.100] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005353)  
It's because sa ating General Appropriations Act, kailangan po ng special provisions sa JA

**[05:34:42.460] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005354)  
para ito ay magamit ng ating OSH,

**[05:34:46.740] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005355)  
para ito ay magamit po natin sa mga susunod na...

**[05:34:51.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005356)  
mag-utilize ng ating mga pondo.

**[05:34:53.660] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005357)  
So, timingly po, hindi pa naman po tapos yung ating...

**[05:34:57.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005358)  
We're just in the middle of the budget deliberations.

**[05:35:00.460] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005359)  
Siguro po, through your representation, ay siguraduhin natin kasi lagi pong nakakaligtaan, unfortunately.

**[05:35:07.620] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005360)  
So ngayon, sana po, with you, stating it for the record,

**[05:35:10.640] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005361)  
na sana po na magkaroon na ng special provision dito para ito ay magamit na ng aming departamento.

**[05:35:15.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005362)  
Madam Speaker, ang mungkahi ng doling ay magdagdag nga ng special provision.

**[05:35:21.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005363)  
Sa ating General Appropriations Bill, para maisama na ang pagbibigay ng authority

**[05:35:27.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005364)  
sa DOLE na gamitin ang 10% ng mga nakolekta ng mga fines

**[05:35:32.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005365)  
para sa operasyon ng Occupational Safety and Health Initiatives

**[05:35:36.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005366)  
kabilang na ang training, education, at health programs.

**[05:35:40.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005367)  
Maari po bang malaman kung bakit ang proposal ng DOLE ay paggamit lamang ng 10%?

**[05:35:46.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005368)  
Hindi ba dapat mas mataas pa yung kinakailangan natin?

**[05:35:50.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005369)  
Para matiyak na mas malawak yung maabot ng mga mahalagang programa na ito?

**[05:35:56.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005370)  
Meron din ba kayong insights kung saan po nagagamit yung mga nakolekta naman itong mga nakaraang taon

**[05:36:02.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005371)  
mula ng mapalakas natin ang OSLO sa ating bansa?

**[05:36:10.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005372)  
Madam Speaker, well initially, tama po kayo na yung 10% lamang yung hinihingi ng DOLE.

**[05:36:17.620] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005373)  
But for the record, pwede naman pong taas.

**[05:36:20.160] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005374)  
But the reason of being which na bakit ito po ay 10% lamang is because

**[05:36:24.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005375)  
one, it's for capacity building muna and may sariling pondo rin yung ibang ahensya tulad ng DA, DOTR, DOH.

**[05:36:34.080] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005376)  
So that's the reason.

**[05:36:35.120] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005377)  
But for the record, as per Department of Labor Employment, pwede naman po itong taasan.

**[05:36:42.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005378)  
Mahalagay ito, Madam Speaker, kasi bagamat may mga kanya-kanyang pondo din yung ibat-ibang mga NGAs natin,

**[05:36:48.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005379)  
siyempre kailangan din natin yung malawak na partisipasyon ng pribadong sektor pagdating sa pagsunod sa OSLO.

**[05:36:56.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005380)  
Kaya bawat piso na mailalaan natin sa programa para dun sa prevention ng kahit anong violations ng OSH standards ay napakalaga.

**[05:37:07.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005381)  
Dahil hindi dapat tayo tumutugon lang kapag may nangyari na aksidente na hindi na dapat nangyayari

**[05:37:15.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005382)  
sa ating mga lugar ng hanap buhay.

**[05:37:18.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005383)  
Hanap buhay nga, di ba? Hindi hanap aksidente o hindi hanap patay sa pinakamalalang pangyayari.

**[05:37:26.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005384)  
Kaya dapat mas mataas yung nilalaan natin na pagtulak sa mga preventive efforts.

**[05:37:34.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005385)  
Ngayon naman, at ang bagay po na yan, ang paglalaan at pagdadagdag ng ganitong special provision

**[05:37:41.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005386)  
ay isusulong ng representasyon na ito na dapat suportahan din ng ating distinguished sponsor.

**[05:37:48.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005387)  
Hindi po tayo masasapat lang sa 10%. Kung pwedeng mas mataas pa, dapat gawin nating suportahan yan.

**[05:37:54.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005388)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Speaker, nais kong magtanong tungkol sa mekanismo ng DOLE para matugunan ang problema ng underemployment sa ating bansa.

**[05:38:06.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005389)  
Sa pinakahuling datos ng PSA, nito lang huling linggo na lumabas, pumalo na sa 12.1% ngayong June 2026.

**[05:38:18.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005390)  
Meron po bang particular na programa para dito ang DOLE?

**[05:38:23.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005391)  
Kasi ito po ay nilapit din natin kahapon sa DepDev na hindi pa sila naaalarma na pagkalaki-laki po ng underemployed sa ating bansa.

**[05:38:35.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005392)  
Kasama dito, Madam Speaker, yung hindi umaabot sa 40 hours kada linggo ang kanilang oras ng trabaho.

**[05:38:44.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005393)  
Kaya po mas maliit yung kanilang kinikita.

**[05:38:48.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005394)  
Nandito rin po, Madam Speaker, yung mga bukod sa kanilang trabaho, naghahanap pa ng ibang mga dagdag, pang racket, para lang makadagdag sa kanila pong income.

**[05:39:00.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005395)  
Meron ba tayong particular programs and initiatives na may kinalaman sa pagtugon sa issue na ito?

**[05:39:07.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005396)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, ako po inipapasalamat at tinanong mo yan.

**[05:39:11.380] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005397)  
Sa pagkat, yun sa ating underemployment, alam naman po natin ang depenisyon.

**[05:39:18.040] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005398)  
Nating kailangan ulitin na yung ating mga kababayan na naginais pa na magkaroon ng dagdagkita.

**[05:39:25.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005399)  
At sa kasalukuyan, ito po ay nasa 12.9 percent.

**[05:39:30.020] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005400)

**[05:39:35.120] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005401)  
At ang inyong katanungan po ay, nakuha ng mga programa ng aming departamento na ito ay para tugunan.

**[05:39:42.880] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005402)  
Alam nyo po, tupad is one of the key factors.

**[05:39:48.040] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005403)  
Na nilalagay po natin yan.

**[05:39:49.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005404)  
Kanina po, tinatanong po tayo ng ating mga kasamahan kung bakit natin na ganong kalaki yung ating pondo is because,

**[05:39:56.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005405)  
alam mo, pag pumunta ka po sa isang lugar na probinsya, ito po ay napakalaking tulong.

**[05:40:02.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005406)  
At yun po ay ang isa sa mga katugunan natin doon sa underemployment.

**[05:40:06.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005407)  
Let's face the reality, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, minsan talaga sa mga probinsya ay kailangan-kailangan ito.

**[05:40:12.520] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005408)  
Pero hindi lang po doon nagtatapos yung ating pagtulong sa pertaining sa underemployment.

**[05:40:16.680] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005409)  
Alam mo, studies have shown that,

**[05:40:18.040] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005410)  
doon sa ating labor force na kalalabas lamang,

**[05:40:21.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005411)

**[05:40:26.480] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005412)

**[05:40:30.100] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005413)

**[05:40:36.200] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005414)

**[05:40:45.200] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005416)

**[05:40:48.040] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005417)

**[05:40:55.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-005418)

**[05:59:08.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005816)

**[05:59:12.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005817)

**[05:59:14.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005818)

**[05:59:16.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005819)

pag-ensure

na that the Board of Investments

appropriate information

**[05:59:58.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005840)  
violation of

**[05:59:59.540] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005841)  
the laws.

**[06:00:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005842)  
for the protection of labor, ay maaari nilang i-cancel yung mga incentives ng specific locators or ng establishments

**[06:00:08.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005843)

**[06:00:13.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005844)  
Ano pong role ng DOLE sa pag-implementa ng Board of Investments ng ganitong klaseng kapangyarihan nila?

**[06:00:21.100] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005845)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague. Tama po yung binanggit po ninyo.

**[06:00:24.640] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005846)  
At I would like to quote sa unang binanggit po ninyo.

**[06:00:28.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005847)  
That ang role po ng Departamento ay ipakita sa ating mga ibang kasama na ahensya na ito po ang findings ng ating Departamento.

**[06:00:38.280] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005848)  
So bari, yung sinasabi po nyo, kung anong role pertaining doon sa kung pwedeng i-cancela,

**[06:00:43.300] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005849)  
ay hindi na po namin sakop ang kapangyarihan ito sapagkat kami po, based on the data that we have,

**[06:00:49.920] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005850)  
and I think we have furnished our distinguished colleague na kumpleto naman po yung aming inspections pertaining to that

**[06:00:57.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005851)  
but to the matter of...

**[06:00:58.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005852)  
So na kami po ay magkakancela, we don't have that jurisdiction, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[06:01:06.620] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005853)  
And I'd like to add, na sisiguraduhin ko po sa inyo, na tayo po ay ginagampanan natin yung ating mga inspections sa lahat ng ating mga ecozones during that time.

**[06:01:15.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005854)  
So to clarify, Madam Speaker, good sponsor, ano po yung role ng DOLE vis-a-vis yung Board of Investments

**[06:01:26.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005855)

**[06:01:28.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005856)

**[06:01:36.160] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005857)

**[06:01:43.200] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005858)  
kung may violations, obligadong ilalagay din po natin yan.

**[06:01:46.920] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005859)  
And we have the records, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, if needed and necessary.

**[06:01:51.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005860)

**[06:01:58.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005861)

**[06:02:02.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005862)

**[06:02:07.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005863)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, we can submit to a report.

**[06:02:10.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005864)  
But gusto ko lang po dagdagan, but in terms of pag-OSH law na po, ito po ay pwedeng patawan na agad ng Departamento.

**[06:02:19.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005865)  
Sa violation ng OSH, for example.

**[06:02:23.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005866)  
Sa OSH po.

**[06:02:23.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005867)  
Gusto natin ma-insure na may...

**[06:02:28.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005868)  
...accountability sa ibang ahensya.

**[06:02:31.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005869)  
Lalo na po, nasa mandato naman po ng ahensya na yun na may accountability kung may violation sa protection,

**[06:02:38.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005870)  
loss of protection of labor, meron po ba itong report na isinusumite, for example,

**[06:02:45.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005871)  
sa mga dapat pong may action on incentives, for example,

**[06:02:50.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005872)  
para hindi lang po titigil tayo sa accountability on OSH law.

**[06:02:58.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005873)  
And other forms of accountability when applicable.

**[06:03:01.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005874)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, yes, sabi ko nga po, ginagawa ng ating Departamento yung role po natin na we furnish everybody a copy.

**[06:03:11.440] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005875)  
And for the appreciation of our distinguished colleague, pwede naman po tayong mag-submit sa inyong tanggapan ng lahat ng aming mga ginawang patungkol po dito.

**[06:03:21.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005876)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, kung maisusumite po dito sa representasyon na ito,

**[06:03:28.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005877)  
kopya ng report, kung paano po tinitiyak ng DOLE na yung modes of accountability niya,

**[06:03:38.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005878)

**[06:03:45.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005879)  
Pero pagbibigay ng impormasyon and coordination with other agencies na maaaring magbigay yung DOLE ng impormasyon sa kanila,

**[06:03:56.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005880)  
at madetalye po sana.

**[06:03:58.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005881)

**[06:03:59.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005882)

**[06:04:08.480] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005883)

**[06:04:39.660] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005888)

**[06:04:43.200] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005889)

**[06:05:20.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005898)  
My last line of questioning, Madam Speaker, good sponsor, is in relation po sa tinanong din po natin sa TESDA,

**[06:05:28.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005899)

**[06:05:38.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005900)

**[06:05:44.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005901)

**[06:05:52.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005902)

**[06:05:58.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005903)

**[06:06:05.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005904)

**[06:06:09.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005905)

**[06:06:16.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005906)

**[06:06:21.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005907)  
upang masiguro ang kanilang tuloy-tuloy na trabaho?

**[06:06:24.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005908)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[06:06:26.840] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005909)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:06:28.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005910)  
before I directly answer your question, let me read first the status of the AI upskilling.

**[06:06:35.800] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005911)  
One, TESDA is calling up its AI skills programs in line with the National AI Upskilling Roadmap

**[06:06:41.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005912)  
through different delivery modalities such as institution-based delivery,

**[06:06:46.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005913)  
enterprise-based training and online courses.

**[06:06:49.280] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005914)  
TESDA has already allocated 53,442 scholarship slots for AI and digital-related skills

**[06:06:56.840] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005915)  
as of August 20, 2026.

**[06:06:58.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005916)  
The context would be that TESDA recognizes the need to adopt our workforce with AI.

**[06:07:06.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005917)  
Kailangan, kailangan na po yan.

**[06:07:08.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005918)  
Estimates vary but different analysis place the number of jobs that will be affected by AI

**[06:07:12.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005919)  
around 25 to 35 percent of jobs.

**[06:07:16.660] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005920)  
That's roughly around 12 million.

**[06:07:18.860] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005921)  
And the National AI Upskilling Roadmap segmentizes the labor force into three clusters

**[06:07:23.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005922)  
that represent three A's in AI.

**[06:07:26.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005923)  
One is AI awareness, AI adoption,

**[06:07:28.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005924)  
and AI advancement.

**[06:07:30.460] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005925)  
This entails different courses from foundational competencies and AI literacy

**[06:07:36.080] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005926)  
to more specialized and advanced courses that will equip Filipinos with skills

**[06:07:40.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005927)  
not only to consume but also to build AI systems.

**[06:07:45.100] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005928)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[06:07:47.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005929)  
So Madam Speaker, good sponsor,

**[06:07:50.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005930)  
ano po yung sagot natin dun sa tanong nasapat po ba ang may 18,

**[06:07:55.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005931)  
or rather ang namention may some 50,000?

**[06:07:57.820] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005932)  
53,000.

**[06:07:58.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005933)  
53,442.

**[06:08:00.120] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005934)  
Madam, doon sa pagbanggit ko nga po kanina,

**[06:08:03.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005935)  
siguro I forgot to mention TESDA.

**[06:08:06.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005936)  
That's the reason na ang unchallenged po sa atin ngayon,

**[06:08:09.360] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005937)  
ang ating hinihingi ng ating ekonomiya.

**[06:08:12.180] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005938)  
Isang role ng DOLE and TESDA,

**[06:08:13.920] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005939)  
TESDA to be specific,

**[06:08:16.100] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005940)  
na i-reskill, i-upskill,

**[06:08:19.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005941)  
and kung sa tanong mo kung sapat na yung binanggit mong 18,000,

**[06:08:24.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005942)  
or in fact, based sa 53,442,

**[06:08:27.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005943)  
sa official stance po ng TESDA,

**[06:08:30.780] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005944)  
ito po ay nabawasan nga yung pondo for 2027,

**[06:08:33.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005945)  
compared to the GAA of 2026.

**[06:08:37.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005946)  
So ang tamang sagot po dyan ay kulang pa po yung ating mga programa,

**[06:08:43.900] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005947)  
and sa ating opening statement po kanina,

**[06:08:46.600] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005948)  
ay binanggit po natin na kung maaari itong madagdagan,

**[06:08:49.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005949)  
ay welcome na welcome po yan sa aming departamento.

**[06:08:53.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005950)  
As a last question, Madam Speaker, good sponsor,

**[06:08:57.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005951)  
or for now,

**[06:08:59.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005952)  
hinihingi po ng kopya sana ang representasyon na ito ng Roadmap,

**[06:09:02.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005953)  
na I assume na medyo fresh or hot off the press pa siya,

**[06:09:07.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005954)  
pero other than that,

**[06:09:09.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005955)  
ano po ba yung mga indicators natin na successful yung upskilling?

**[06:09:13.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005956)  
Yung ilan pong siguro inaasahan natin,

**[06:09:16.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005957)  
yung promotion sa trabaho,

**[06:09:18.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005958)  
increased salary,

**[06:09:20.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005959)  
siguro mas mabilis na turnout, etc.

**[06:09:24.600] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005960)  
Ano po ba ang mga indicators na ito?

**[06:09:27.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005961)  
Magandang tanong, to justify na kami po ay ang TESDA po ay humihingi po ng karagdagang budget for that.

**[06:09:34.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005962)  
One, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague,

**[06:09:36.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005963)  
I'd like to state for the record,

**[06:09:39.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005964)  
na lahat po nang dumaan sa tech-voc path,

**[06:09:43.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005965)  
ay based on the results na ginagawa po natin,

**[06:09:47.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005966)  
ay meron pong 83.4% employability.

**[06:09:50.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005967)  
Ibig sabihin, pag ikaw po ay dumaan sa skills training sa TESDA,

**[06:09:55.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005968)  
ay mataas, o napakataas,

**[06:09:57.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005969)  
ng chance for employability.

**[06:09:59.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005970)  
And also, it goes to show sa ating certification program,

**[06:10:03.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005971)  
na if it is applicable to or acceptable to the distinguished colleague,

**[06:10:10.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005972)  
ay marami po na tayo nagawa dyan,

**[06:10:12.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005973)  
and we can definitely furnish you

**[06:10:14.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005974)  
of all that necessary reports pertaining to certification,

**[06:10:17.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005975)  
and what I've said, the 83.4% employability

**[06:10:20.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005976)  
ng mga dumadaan sa tech-voc path po natin sa ating bansang Pilipinas.

**[06:10:24.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005977)  
Hintayin po natin yung ulat na yun.

**[06:10:26.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005978)  
Siguro sana po sa lunes ay maisabay siya doon sa submissions as well ng DOLE.

**[06:10:33.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005979)  
Ang huli na lang po nating manifestation ay up until now,

**[06:10:37.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005980)  
mataas ang anxiety ng mga tao tungkol sa layoffs na dulot ng AI.

**[06:10:43.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005981)  
At kung we ride the trend of upskilling,

**[06:10:47.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005982)  
ang naishare po so far ay hindi rin po nakikita sa indicators na may job security dito.

**[06:10:55.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005983)  
So, nonetheless, Madam Speaker, good sponsor,

**[06:10:58.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005984)  
we'd like to register na ito yung anxiety at ito yung hirap na pinakaharap ng ating mga BPO workers.

**[06:11:05.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005985)  
At beyond upskilling, no, gusto natin tuloy-tuloy na may sagot doon sa tanong na paano ba i-ensure

**[06:11:11.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005986)  
na may disenteng trabaho, na may nakabubuhay na sahod ang mga manggagawang Pilipino.

**[06:11:16.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005987)  
Yun lang po, Madam Speaker, maraming salamat po. Thank you, good sponsor.

**[06:11:20.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005988)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker. Thank you, distinguished colleague.

**[06:11:23.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-005989)  
And yes, we will submit on Monday together with the DOLE and TESDA reports, Distinguished Colleague. Maraming salamat po.

**[06:11:30.220] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-005990)  
Majority Leader?

**[06:11:32.220] Floor Leader** (asr-005991)  
Madam Speaker, I'd like to acknowledge our last interpellator of the day,

**[06:11:37.220] Floor Leader** (asr-005992)  
the Honorable Perci Cendaña from the Akbayan Party-list.

**[06:11:41.220] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-005993)  
Representative Perci Cendaña of Akbayan Party-list is hereby recognized for his interpellation.

**[06:11:46.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005994)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker. Magandang umaga po. Ginoong sponsor at sa buong DOLE family.

**[06:11:52.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005995)  
At siyempre kasama dyan, special mention, ang paborito nating TESDA.

**[06:11:57.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005996)  
At ngayon po, pinag-uusapan natin ang budget ng Department of Labor and Employment.

**[06:12:04.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005997)  
At pangunahin dito yung usapin ng workers' rights and welfare.

**[06:12:08.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005998)  
But the irony is not lost on me. Marami po mga manggagawa tayo dito sa loob ng Kongreso, sa ating mga opisina, at mismo po sa DOLE, ang Puyat na ngayong araw na ito.

**[06:12:21.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-005999)  
And while naniniwala tayo na dapat walang tulugan para sa transparency and accountability,

**[06:12:27.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006000)  
sa dulo po dapat huwag din nating pabayaan ang kapakanan ng ating mga manggagawa.

**[06:12:32.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006001)  
Let me begin with a humble appeal to the leadership of the House of Representatives.

**[06:12:37.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006002)  
Natiyakin po natin yung kapakanan, kalusugan ng ating mga manggagawa, ang kanilang well-being.

**[06:12:43.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006003)  
Hindi po talaga makatarungan ang ganitong uri ng diskurso sa ganitong alangan ng oras na ng madaling araw.

**[06:12:51.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006004)  
And the irony of it all, we're talking about workers' rights and welfare now.

**[06:12:56.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006005)  
Balik lang po ako, Madam Speaker, ginaong sponsor doon sa kaninang exchange between the good sponsor and Representative Elly tungkol po sa nangyaring September 10 NWPC meeting.

**[06:13:11.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006006)  
Allegedly, may nangyaring NWPC meeting noong September 10. At nabanggit din po kanina na isang dahilan kaya po lumipat ng kwarto ay dahil daw po mayroong disruption.

**[06:13:21.220] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006007)  
Can the good sponsor elaborate further on this claim about disruptions or disruptors?

**[06:13:29.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006008)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, well, the process sa wage setting, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, there's a reason na bakit po may proseso.

**[06:13:42.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006009)  
One, nabanggit po natin leading to the September 10, was that there was already a public hearing.

**[06:13:51.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006010)

**[06:13:55.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006011)

**[06:14:00.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006012)  
Pertaining doon po sa sinasabing September 10, tandaan po ninyo, yung ito na po ay nagpapatukoy doon sa Wage Order No. 28 na dapat ay affirmation.

**[06:14:14.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006013)  
And for the record, sabi ko nga po kanina, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, the reason of being wage na ito po ay ating nabanggit.

**[06:14:21.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006014)  
Sapagkat, yung mga miyembro dapat ng ating na kung saan na, nasa affirmation doon sa ating wage order, ay dapat nandun na po.

**[06:14:30.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006015)  
Nandun din yun sa binanggit ko na yung labor sector. Unfortunately, yung isa pong miyembro ay wala po.

**[06:14:37.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006016)  
And we have the minutes to prove so, na yun lamang po dapat talaga yung mga naroon.

**[06:14:43.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006017)  
So in short, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague,

**[06:14:47.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006018)  
yung board po mismo yun nandun. And siguro, you just don't take my word for it.

**[06:14:55.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006019)  
Kanina nga po, I've asked permission from the good secretariat, tandaan po natin yung

**[06:15:00.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006020)  
yung dapat yung pag nagdi-desisyon na

**[06:15:02.560] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006021)  
o pag mag-a-affirm na ang NWPC,

**[06:15:06.160] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006022)

**[06:15:11.380] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006023)  
Pero kami po, sabi ko nga po, on the record,

**[06:15:14.580] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006024)  
kasi ganun po kami kakumpiyansa,

**[06:15:16.060] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006025)

**[06:15:18.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006026)  
Yan po ay may para at least to appease everybody.

**[06:15:21.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006027)  
At the same time, para ipakita

**[06:15:23.440] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006028)  
na kami ang Department of Labor and Employment

**[06:15:26.080] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006029)  
pertaining to that September 10 meeting

**[06:15:28.240] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006030)  
sa affirmation ng Wage Order 28

**[06:15:30.360] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006031)  
ay walang tinatago at the same time

**[06:15:32.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006032)  
para sinigurado rin po natin

**[06:15:34.280] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006033)  
na yung Wage Order No. 28

**[06:15:35.880] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006034)  
ay ma-affirm natin,

**[06:15:38.260] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006035)

**[06:15:40.980] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006036)  
and hopefully ma-implement

**[06:15:43.020] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006037)

**[06:15:46.000] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006038)  
to be implemented on September 26 of this year.

**[06:15:49.340] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006039)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[06:15:51.580] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006040)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[06:15:54.840] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006041)

**[06:15:57.480] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006042)

**[06:16:00.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006043)

**[06:16:03.000] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006044)

**[06:16:05.420] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006045)

**[06:16:11.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006046)  
Well,

**[06:16:15.120] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006047)  
let it, ilalagay ko po sa konteksto.

**[06:16:18.800] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006048)

**[06:16:21.500] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006049)

**[06:16:26.700] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006050)  
Nandun na po.

**[06:16:27.480] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006051)

**[06:16:32.320] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006052)

**[06:16:34.220] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006053)

**[06:16:36.880] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006054)

**[06:16:42.020] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006056)

**[06:16:44.720] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006057)  
Now, I have it on

**[06:16:47.440] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006058)  
there's labor leaders na mga pumasok

**[06:16:53.400] Rep. Rommel Rico T. Angara** (asr-006059)

ito,

Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[06:17:09.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006067)  
Just to reiterate, Madam Speaker,

**[06:17:11.380] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006068)

**[06:17:12.280] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006069)

**[06:17:13.700] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006070)

na malaman ang katotohanan

**[06:17:22.720] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-006076)  
at magpahayag po
